### Icy blue dragon breathes fire, then gazes into a gold mirror

> 🐉 A curious dragon discovers a mirror—and falls in love with its own reflection! 😂💙🪞 A Dragon’s Life | Episode 3 Created with Seedance 2.5 ✨ Prompt below 👇

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/fa15f3fb2ee1.jpg" width="600" alt="Icy blue dragon breathes fire, then gazes into a gold mirror">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-create-a-30-second-ultra-realistic-cinematic-fantasy-video-16-9-widescreen-wi-4916aa2713af?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @ZunairaSaeedAi | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/ZunairaSaeedAi/status/2123257938609586526) | **公開日:** 2026-11-19 | **ヒート:** 93

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（60 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Create a 30-second ultra-realistic cinematic fantasy video, 16:9 widescreen, with consistent characters, natural motion, detailed facial expressions, realistic physics, smooth cinematic camera movements, and high-end CGI quality.

CHARACTER CONSISTENCY:

A cute small fantasy dragon with pearly white scales, icy blue scales, vivid sapphire-blue crystal spikes along its head, neck and back, two curved horns, bright blue eyes, small sharp teeth, large blue membranous wings, and a long tail. The dragon has an expressive, adorable face and realistic detailed scales.

A young blonde woman wearing a medieval fantasy dress with a cream-colored blouse, puffed sleeves, and a long dark burgundy-brown skirt. Her hairstyle, face, clothing and proportions must remain consistent throughout the video.

ENVIRONMENT:

A beautiful sunlit countryside meadow with vibrant green grass, rolling hills, a low stone wall, a rustic stone cottage in the background, and distant trees. A tall freestanding oval mirror with an ornate antique gold frame and decorative curved feet stands upright on the grass. The mirror is reflective and remains in exactly the same position throughout the scene.

SHOT-BY-SHOT ACTION AND CAMERA DIRECTIONS:

0–2 seconds:
Begin with a cinematic wide shot of the countryside. The young woman stands beside the tall ornate golden mirror. The blue-and-white crystal dragon walks into the scene from the left, approaching the woman and the mirror. Gentle natural camera movement, realistic walking motion, warm sunlight.

2–3 seconds:
Cut to an extreme close-up of the dragon's face. Its vivid blue eyes look forward with curiosity. Show highly detailed white scales, blue crystalline spikes, subtle breathing, and natural eye movement.

3–5 seconds:
Cut to a frontal medium-wide shot. The dragon lowers its body, spreads both large wings outward, raises its head, and opens its mouth in a dramatic roar. Its wings and body move naturally. The camera captures the full dragon's posture.

5–7 seconds:
Cut to a side close-up. The dragon turns its head toward the woman and releases a powerful bright orange stream of fire from its mouth. The fire extends horizontally across the foreground toward the woman and mirror. The woman reacts in surprise, raising both arms in front of her face and leaning backward. The fire creates realistic orange light, heat distortion, and glowing reflections. Do not show injuries or destruction.

7–9 seconds:
The fire disappears. Cut to a wide side shot of the dragon, the woman, and the standing mirror. The dragon lowers its head and begins moving closer to the mirror. The woman remains beside it. Keep the countryside and cottage consistent.

9–12 seconds:
The dragon slowly approaches the mirror, looking directly at its reflection. Use a medium-wide composition with the dragon in the foreground and the ornate mirror clearly visible. The mirror accurately reflects the dragon's face and body.

12–14 seconds:
Cut to a close shot framed through the golden mirror. Show the dragon's reflection looking back at itself. It blinks slowly, tilts its head slightly, and studies its own face with curiosity. The reflected movements must match the real dragon precisely.

14–16 seconds:
Close-up of the dragon beside the mirror. It turns its head slightly, its blue eyes widen, and it looks fascinated by the reflection. Smooth cinematic camera movement, shallow depth of field, realistic sunlight on the crystal spikes.

16–18 seconds:
Cut to a wider shot showing the dragon and mirror together. The dragon moves its head closer to the mirror, opens its mouth in a playful surprised expression, then relaxes. Keep the woman in the background.

18–24 seconds:
Cinematic medium-wide shot from a slightly low angle. The dragon stands proudly in front of the mirror, gradually raises its head and opens its large blue wings. Warm sunlight shines from behind, creating beautiful rim lighting around the wings, horns, and crystal spikes. The dragon admires its reflection with a proud, curious, playful expression. The camera remains smooth and cinematic, with subtle movement.

24–26 seconds:
Cut to a close-up of the dragon's reflection inside the oval mirror. The dragon gently closes its eyes and leans its face toward the mirror. Its expression becomes peaceful and affectionate.

26–28 seconds:
Extreme close-up through the mirror. The young woman's hand enters gently from the side and touches the dragon's nose with her index finger. The dragon keeps its eyes closed and responds with a soft, affectionate expression. The interaction is gentle, natural, and emotionally warm. Detailed facial animation and realistic contact.

28–30 seconds:
Finish with a wide cinematic shot of the dragon, the young woman, and the ornate mirror together in the sunlit meadow. The dragon stands beside the mirror with its long blue tail extending behind it. The woman stands next to the dragon. The camera gently pulls back, showing the cottage, stone wall, green hills, and warm sunlight. End with a peaceful cinematic fade to black.

VISUAL STYLE:
Photorealistic fantasy CGI, cinematic film quality, realistic textures, detailed dragon scales, expressive blue eyes, realistic wing anatomy, natural character motion, physically accurate reflections, warm daylight, soft shadows, beautiful golden-hour highlights, cinematic depth of field, smooth camera transitions, consistent character identity, high detail.

STRICT CONTINUITY:
The same dragon, woman, dress, mirror, cottage, meadow, lighting direction, and landscape must remain consistent in every shot. The mirror must always reflect the actual dragon in the correct position. The dragon's movements and reflection must synchronize perfectly.

Do not add extra characters, additional dragons, extra mirrors, scene changes, random objects, text, subtitles, logos, watermarks, inconsistent dragon anatomy, distorted faces, flickering, abrupt camera movements, or unrelated actions. Follow the exact chronological sequence described above.
```

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（227 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
THE PINK PAINT CLASSROOM CHAOS — KOREAN SCHOOL COMEDY

Duration: 30.6 seconds
Aspect Ratio: 16:9

Style: Photorealistic Korean live-action school comedy, cinematic slapstick, realistic physical action.

1. CHARACTER REFERENCE AND CONSISTENCY

Main Girl — Black-haired Student

A young adult Korean female student with shoulder-length straight black hair, soft bangs, and a calm expression. She wears a beige school blazer with a school emblem, a navy V-neck sweater vest, a white collared shirt, a burgundy necktie, a dark navy plaid pleated skirt, white socks, and black loafers.

She carries a light peach-colored drink cup with a green lid and straw. Throughout the chaotic scene, she remains surprisingly calm and keeps drinking her beverage.

Preserve her exact face, black hairstyle, beige blazer, navy vest, tie, skirt, and drink cup in every shot.

Second Girl — Long Brown-haired Student

A young Korean female student with long straight chestnut-brown hair. She wears a white collared shirt with a large dark plaid bow, a dark plaid pleated skirt, white knee-high socks, and black school shoes.

Initially she looks playful and mischievous. During the prank, her white shirt, hair, and body become covered in bright pink paint. Her appearance gradually changes into a completely pink, paint-covered student.

Male Student — Blond-haired Boy

A young Korean male student with short bleached-blond hair, wearing a beige school blazer, navy sweater vest, white shirt, dark tie, and dark trousers. His face and clothing become covered in bright pink paint during the chaos.

Other Students

Several Korean male and female students in matching beige school blazers, navy sweaters, white shirts, ties, and dark plaid skirts or trousers. They watch, laugh, react, and become involved in the classroom chaos.

2. LOCATION AND ENVIRONMENT

A large Korean school art classroom with:

- Green-painted floor.
- Large wooden worktables and wooden chairs.
- Whiteboard at the front.
- Large windows allowing natural daylight.
- Art easels and painting equipment.
- A classroom entrance with double doors.
- Large paint containers and buckets.
- Brushes, art supplies, and scattered classroom materials.

Bright pink paint spreads across the green floor during the incident. Wooden furniture is moved and overturned during the chaos.

Maintain the same classroom architecture, windows, doors, furniture, and lighting throughout.

3. SHOT-BY-SHOT SEQUENCE

SHOT 1 — 0–1.5 SECONDS: THE MISCHIEVOUS GIRL

Begin with a close-up of the long brown-haired Korean schoolgirl wearing a white shirt and large dark plaid bow.

She stands inside the art classroom, smiling mischievously and covering part of her mouth with one hand as if trying not to laugh.

Other students are visible out of focus behind her. Use a shallow-depth-of-field cinematic close-up.

SHOT 2 — 1.5–3 SECONDS: THE TRAP AT THE DOOR

Cut to a rear wide shot of the classroom entrance.

The black-haired girl wearing a beige school blazer and navy sweater vest walks toward the doorway while calmly drinking from her peach-colored cup through a straw.

A large metal bucket filled with bright pink paint is positioned above the entrance. As she approaches and passes underneath, the bucket begins to fall and tilt.

Show the bucket moving downward above the doorway while the girl continues holding her drink.

SHOT 3 — 3–5 SECONDS: THE PINK PAINT EXPLOSION

Cut to a low-angle shot from inside the classroom.

The pink paint bucket falls and spills its contents dramatically. Bright pink paint splashes across the green classroom floor and nearby students.

The long brown-haired girl and other students react with shock. One student crouches and another falls backward. A large puddle of pink paint spreads across the floor.

Show the bucket tumbling through the air and the pink liquid splashing realistically.

SHOT 4 — 5–7 SECONDS: THE CALM GIRL

Cut to a close-up of the black-haired girl holding her peach-colored drink.

She stands completely calm, takes another sip through the straw, and looks forward with a neutral expression.

She says casually:

"You're welcome."

Keep the English subtitle exactly as shown in the reference style.

The pink paint-covered classroom remains softly visible behind her.

SHOT 5 — 7–10 SECONDS: THE PINK-PAINTED GIRL REACTS

Cut to a close-up of the long brown-haired girl.

Her white school shirt is splattered with bright pink paint, including her shoulder, chest, and hair.

She looks down at the paint on her uniform, then raises her face and shouts angrily with an exaggerated comedic expression.

Her long brown hair and plaid bow remain recognizable despite the paint.

SHOT 6 — 10–13 SECONDS: THE PINK-FACED BOY

Cut to a medium action shot.

A blond-haired male student with his face covered in bright pink paint suddenly charges forward inside the classroom.

The black-haired girl quickly ducks and moves out of his way while still holding her drink cup.

The boy's arms extend outward as he loses control of his movement. Nearby wooden tables and chairs are pushed and disturbed.

Use fast but readable physical comedy.

SHOT 7 — 13–17 SECONDS: CLASSROOM FIGHT AND CHAOS

A wider shot reveals the classroom in complete disorder.

The black-haired girl dodges an approaching student. Another student is knocked backward onto a wooden table, causing it to slide and tip.

Bright pink paint covers a large section of the green floor. Students run and stumble around the room.

The girl continues moving through the chaos while holding her peach-colored drink.

Maintain clear positions and realistic physical movement.

SHOT 8 — 17–20 SECONDS: STUDENTS COLLIDE

Cut to a wide shot near the center of the classroom.

Two female students in beige school blazers and dark plaid skirts collide while struggling with each other. Their feet slide through the pink paint.

One student loses her balance and falls. Their skirts and white socks move naturally during the fall.

Other students react from behind the desks.

SHOT 9 — 20–23 SECONDS: THE BLACK-HAIRED GIRL WATCHES

Cut to a medium shot of the black-haired girl standing near the classroom entrance.

She casually drinks from her peach-colored cup while watching the long brown-haired girl covered in pink paint.

The pink-haired-looking student is down on the floor beside a fallen metal paint container. Bright pink paint is spread across the floor and her clothes.

The black-haired girl remains completely calm.

SHOT 10 — 23–27 SECONDS: THE SAME HAIR COLOR JOKE

Cut to a low-angle medium close-up.

The pink-paint-covered girl is sitting or kneeling on the floor, her long hair completely coated in bright pink paint.

The black-haired girl stands beside her, holding her drink in one hand.

She gently picks up a strand of her own black hair with the other hand and compares it with the pink-painted girl's hair.

She says:

"Wow, our hair's the same color."

Display the English subtitle in the same style as the reference video.

The pink-covered girl looks upward at her while the black-haired girl remains expressionless and casually drinks.

SHOT 11 — 27–30.6 SECONDS: THE FINAL CLASSROOM CHAOS

The black-haired girl calmly walks toward the classroom exit, still holding her drink.

Behind her, the pink-painted girl remains on the floor surrounded by bright pink paint.

Cut to a wide view of the entire classroom. Several students and male classmates are now down on their knees, their hands touching the pink paint spread across the floor.

Some students are covered in pink paint, and the classroom furniture is scattered around.

End with a wide cinematic shot showing the pink-covered classroom, the students kneeling in the mess, and the black-haired girl remaining calm.

4. VISUAL EFFECTS AND PHYSICAL DETAILS

The bright pink paint must look thick, wet, glossy, and realistic.

Show:

- Pink paint splashing from the falling bucket.
- Paint spreading across the green floor.
- Pink stains appearing on uniforms, hair, and faces.
- The metal bucket rolling across the floor.
- Wooden tables and chairs moving during the chaos.
- Realistic slipping, falling, and recovering movements.

Do not turn the paint into magical energy or supernatural effects.

5. CAMERA AND CINEMATOGRAPHY

Use photorealistic Korean school-comedy cinematography.

- Close-up of the mischievous brown-haired girl.
- Rear wide shot of the classroom entrance.
- Low-angle shot of the falling paint bucket.
- Close-up of the black-haired girl drinking.
- Facial close-up of the pink-covered girl.
- Dynamic medium shots during the classroom chaos.
- Wide shots showing students and furniture.
- Final low-angle comparison of black and pink hair.
- Wide concluding classroom shot.

Use natural daylight from the classroom windows, realistic skin textures, natural hair movement, accurate liquid physics, and cinematic depth of field.

6. AUDIO AND DIALOGUE

Include:

- Quiet classroom ambience.
- Bucket falling and metal impact.
- Loud pink paint splashing.
- Students gasping, laughing, and shouting.

- Wooden desks and chairs moving.

- Final dialogue: "Wow, our hair's the same color."

7. STRICT NEGATIVE PROMPT

No zombies, no meteors, no fantasy powers, no blue lightning, no guns, no weapons, no outdoor scenes, no changing classroom layout, no changing school uniforms, no changing character identities, no changing black-haired girl's hairstyle, no changing brown-haired girl's hairstyle, no random hair colors, no additional main characters, no duplicated students, no extra limbs, no distorted faces, no cartoon style, no anime style, no random text, no watermark.

FINAL OUTPUT: A coherent 30.6-second photorealistic Korean school-comedy video. ### Seedance 2.5 Hyperreal AI Live Wallpaper: Outfit-Changing Heroine in One Take

> @cnyzgkc reveals that an AI live wallpaper video with 290K views was made with Seedance 2.5, improving on a prompt from @johnAGI168. A 29-second fixed-camera scene: a woman on a black leather sofa changes into three campus outfits on off-screen male voice commands in one take.

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/f16c9f956a8d.jpg" width="600" alt="Seedance 2.5 Hyperreal AI Live Wallpaper: Outfit-Changing Heroine in One Take">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-ai-cabf3749d5b6?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

时长29秒，比例16:9横屏，语言中文。
人物参考：仅参考图片1锁定五官、发型、肤色和人物身份；服装严格按下文变化。人物要求：成年女主。全程同一人物，不换脸、不改变身材比例；提高权重，优先死死锁住五官、发型、肤色、身份。

整体风格：超真实AI动态桌面互动视频，模拟电脑全屏桌面录屏。底部保留简洁任务栏，左上角少量桌面图标；画面主体是一张会实时互动的动态壁纸。黑色皮质沙发，冷色柔光，深色背景。固定机位，全程一镜连续，不切镜、不推拉。女主主要位于画面右侧，左侧留给语音字幕界面。

字幕界面（保留，覆盖「禁止文字」约束）：字幕必须出现于画面左侧中部，不能放在底部。纤细现代无衬线中文字体，中等字号，无底框，轻微柔光和阴影。女主说话：淡紫色文字，左侧显示扬声器图标。男声说话：白色文字，左侧显示麦克风图标。字幕下方始终有一条细小白色音频波形，随声音实时跳动。所有字幕跟随发音逐字输入，约每字0.08—0.12秒，像键盘正在手动打字；当前句说完后停留约0.4秒，再整体淡出。不能整句突然出现，不能提前显示后面的字。

服装设计（三套造型统一只改领口：低圆领/浅V领，微露事业线，其余保持完整遮盖）：
初始造型：黑白横条纹修身短款七分袖上衣（低圆领/浅V领，微露事业线，其余完整遮盖），黑色低腰抽绳休闲长裤，银色细项链。
第一套学院风：上衣为低圆领/浅V领、微露事业线、其余完整遮盖，蓝黑格纹百褶短裙，白色过膝袜，白色十字装饰袜带，整体清爽俏皮。
第二套学院风：黑色修身西装外套、白色衬衫（衬衫低圆领/浅V领，微露事业线，其余完整遮盖）、酒红格纹领带、酒红黑色格纹百褶短裙、浅肤色薄丝袜、黑色乐福鞋，精致成熟。

SINGLE CONTINUOUS SHOT（00:00-00:29）

00:00-00:02.80
女主穿初始造型坐在黑色皮沙发右侧，腿上抱着黑色靠枕。她双手轻轻整理头发，随后看向镜头，露出亲切、略带俏皮的微笑。
女主自然开口：「说吧……今天想看什么？」
字幕逐字显示：「说吧……
今天想看什么？」

00:03.00-00:03.80
男主始终不露脸，仅有画外男声，语气直接而期待：「想看JK。」
女主安静听着，笑容稍微加深，轻轻点头。
字幕逐字显示：「想看JK」

00:04.70-00:06.00
女主爽快回答：「好，等我一下。」
她把怀里的靠枕放到一旁，右手撑住沙发准备起身。
字幕逐字显示：「好，等我一下。」

00:06.00-00:08.80
女主快速起身。由于机位固定且距离较近，她的腰部和腿部从镜头前向上掠过，脸部离开画面上沿；随后向右侧走出画面。动作带轻快的「嗖」声，但不能瞬移、不能直接变形。

00:08.80-00:11.50
换装完全在画外完成。沙发上多留约一秒空镜。伴随轻快的亮相音效，女主穿第一套蓝色学院风造型从右侧重新走入。她站得离镜头较近，画面主要呈现腰部、格纹裙摆和过膝袜，头部自然超出画面上沿。
女主带着展示新造型的俏皮语气说：「当当……老公……怎么样？」
字幕逐字显示：「当当……
老公……怎么样？」

00:11.70-00:12.50
男主画外音稍作思考，满意地回答：「嗯……不错。」
女主保持站姿，轻微左右转动腰身，让格纹裙摆自然摆动。
字幕逐字显示：「嗯……不错」

00:12.90-00:14.40
女主突然想起另一套造型，轻快地说：「等等，还有一套。」
说完立即转身向右侧离画，裙摆随动作摆动。
字幕逐字显示：「等等，还有一套」

00:14.40-00:17.40
换装完全在画外完成。沙发上多留约一秒空镜。随后响起一次短促流畅的换装「嗖」声，女主穿第二套酒红学院风造型从右侧走入。固定镜头先看到黑色西装下摆、酒红格纹裙、丝袜和乐福鞋，服装必须真实完整，不能在行走中融化或改变。

00:17.40-00:19.00
女主停在镜头右侧，略微调整站姿，让西装下摆和裙褶自然落下，询问：「这套呢？」
字幕逐字显示：「这套呢？」

00:19.50-00:20.50
男主画外音立即赞赏：「这套也好看。」
女主听完轻轻笑一下，身体开始降低准备坐下。
字幕逐字显示：「这套也好看」

00:20.50-00:24.80
伴随轻微衣料摩擦声，女主迅速而自然地坐回沙发。她将黑色靠枕重新放在腿上，整理两侧头发和领带，随后微微侧身靠住沙发。镜头重新完整拍到她的脸。她始终看着镜头，表情自信、亲近、略带捉弄感。

00:25.00-00:28.20
女主抱住靠枕，先露出得意的小笑容，然后对镜头说：「机会用完啦。老公想留哪套呢？」
「机会用完啦」语气像宣布游戏规则；「老公想留哪套呢」稍微放慢，尾音上扬，带亲密询问感。
字幕逐字显示：「机会用完啦。
老公想留哪套呢？」

00:28.20-00:29.00
字幕完整停留。女主保持眼神交流，轻轻偏头，嘴角维持俏皮微笑。波形逐渐归于平静，画面自然结束。

音频：女主真实现场人声，口型精确；男主为镜头外中文男声，不露脸。保留轻微电子氛围音乐（对白时自动降低，权重提高）、换装「嗖」声、亮相提示音、衣料摩擦声，所有对白清楚可辨。

表演约束：女主不是夸张卖萌。微笑、点头、整理头发、展示服装均自然克制；开场亲切，第一次换装俏皮，第二次换装更自信，结尾带轻微捉弄感。男声说话时女主必须闭嘴并自然聆听。

50-SECOND VIDEO PROMPT — GTA / AAA CINEMATIC STYLE

Photorealistic AAA cinematic short, GTA-style urban energy: saturated colors, punchy contrast, dynamic camera (low heroic angles, smooth tracking, brief crane and push-ins), rich environmental detail, slightly larger-than-life character presence, filmic grain, sharp fur and fabric simulation. Duration exactly 50 seconds. Natural morning light mixed with colorful market stall canopies. Busy, funny, lively tone. English subtitles at the bottom. Japanese dialogue in a warm elderly Kansai-accented female voice for the grandmother.  CAST (use these exact descriptions every time they appear): - An elderly Japanese grandmother (short gray hair in a loose bun, wrinkled kind face, purple-pink floral vest over a patterned blouse, dark pants) - A giant tanuki (raccoon-dog), about 6.5 feet tall standing on hind legs, thick brown-grey fur, round belly, mischievous dark-ringed eyes, wearing a slightly stained vendor apron, walking upright like a person - A tiny brown sparrow perched on the grandmother’s shoulder - A grumpy middle-aged Japanese fishmonger in a rubber apron  ---  0:00–0:07 — ESTABLISHING   Wide crane shot descending into a crowded Japanese open-air morning market. Fish stalls, vegetable crates, hanging flowers, steam, shouting vendors, bicycles, plastic bags. Camera finds an elderly Japanese grandmother (short gray hair in a loose bun, wrinkled kind face, purple-pink floral vest over a patterned blouse, dark pants) walking into the market carrying an empty basket. A tiny brown sparrow is already perched on her right shoulder. She looks delighted and a little overwhelmed.  Dialogue (grandmother):   「今日は安うてええ日やわ。」   Subtitle: “Prices are good today. Dangerous for me.”  ---  0:07–0:16 — FISH STALL**   Medium tracking shot. An elderly Japanese grandmother (short gray hair in a loose bun, wrinkled kind face, purple-pink floral vest over a patterned blouse, dark pants) stops at a fish stall piled with ice and silver fish. The grumpy fishmonger slaps a huge fish onto the scale. The tiny sparrow hops down, pecks a piece of dried squid, and flies back to her shoulder. She notices and gently scolds it without real anger.  Dialogue (grandmother):   「あんたまた盗んでるやないの。恥ずかしいわ。」   Subtitle: “You little thief. That’s embarrassing.”  The fishmonger frowns.   Fishmonger: 「ばあさん、そんなに持って帰れんやろ。」   Subtitle: “Grandma, you can’t carry all that.”  ---  **0:16–0:28 — TANUKI ARRIVES AND BARGAINS**   Low heroic angle. The crowd parts. A giant tanuki in a vendor apron walks upright through the aisle, heavy and confident, GTA-style presence. He stops beside an elderly Japanese grandmother (short gray hair in a loose bun, wrinkled kind face, purple-pink floral vest over a patterned blouse, dark pants), looks at the fishmonger’s price, then slaps a paw on the counter.  Tanuki (deep, playful male voice):   「その値段、冗談やろ。半分でええやろ。」   Subtitle: “That price? You’re joking. Half.”  The fishmonger argues, then reluctantly nods. The giant tanuki grins. An elderly Japanese grandmother (short gray hair in a loose bun, wrinkled kind face, purple-pink floral vest over a patterned blouse, dark pants) covers her mouth, laughing.  Dialogue (grandmother):   「もう、あんたまで出てこんでもええのに。」   Subtitle: “You didn’t have to jump in… but thank you.”  ---  0:28–0:40 — CARRYING EVERYTHING   Smooth side-tracking shot through the busy market. The giant tanuki now carries two overflowing wooden crates of fish, vegetables, and flowers on his shoulders like it’s nothing. An elderly Japanese grandmother (short gray hair in a loose bun, wrinkled kind face, purple-pink floral vest over a patterned blouse, dark pants) walks beside him, still holding her small basket. The tiny sparrow keeps darting down to steal small snacks from open stalls and returning to her shoulder. Shoppers stare and smile. Dynamic market chaos in the background: steam, shouting, hanging lanterns, wet pavement reflections.  Dialogue (grandmother, amused):   「箱二つも買うんやったらええ加減にせな。肩の子も手伝いすぎや。」   Subtitle: “Two crates is too much. And you on my shoulder, stop helping so much.”  ---  0:40–0:50 — FINAL BEAT   The trio stops at the edge of the market near a narrow street. The giant tanuki sets the crates down carefully, then stands proud with paws on his hips. Close-up of an elderly Japanese grandmother (short gray hair in a loose bun, wrinkled kind face, purple-pink floral vest over a patterned blouse, dark pants) looking up at him, then at the tiny sparrow on her shoulder. She reaches up and gently taps the sparrow’s head, then pats the tanuki’s massive forearm.  Dialogue (grandmother, soft and affectionate):   「ほんま、困った相棒やわ。けど…助かった。帰ろか。」   Subtitle: “What a pair of troublemakers. …Still, you saved me. Let’s go home.”  Final shot: slow push-in. The giant tanuki picks the crates up again. An elderly Japanese grandmother (short gray hair in a loose bun, wrinkled kind face, purple-pink floral vest over a patterned blouse, dark pants) walks ahead. The tiny sparrow settles on her shoulder as they leave the colorful market toward the street. Hold 1.5 seconds, then cut to black.  ---  TECH / STYLE LOCKS   GTA AAA cinematic look: vibrant market colors, strong sunlight shafts through stall roofs, wet ground reflections, dense extras, slightly exaggerated scale on the giant tanuki, ultra-detailed fur, realistic plastic bags and ice, handheld-feeling but stabilized tracking. Main subject: young Indonesian woman, early 20s, natural everyday appearance, faded mustard-yellow oversized cotton t-shirt, brown high-waisted linen wrap skirt reaching just below the knee, simple brown leather slide sandals, thin gold stud earrings, dark wavy hair in a messy low bun with loose wispy strands framing her face. Realistic warm brown skin texture, minimal natural makeup, warm and approachable demeanor. Maintain consistent identity, clothing, hairstyle, and appearance throughout the entire video.

Location: Authentic Indonesian residential neighborhood (gang) during a calm late morning. Narrow paved alleys, low-rise houses with open front terraces, terracotta roof tiles, potted frangipani and bougainvillea, laundry on bamboo drying racks, parked motorbikes along plastered walls, overhead utility wires, banana and coconut trees casting dappled moving shadows, quiet tropical residential atmosphere. No modern stores, billboards, cafés, or crowds.

Visual Style: Ultra-realistic documentary realism. Genuine candid behavior. Natural body language. Unscripted slice-of-life feeling. Strong environmental authenticity. Rich real-world details and believable human motion.

Camera Style: Early-2000s consumer DV camcorder aesthetic. Friend casually recording everyday moments. Heavy handheld shake, imperfect framing, frequent autofocus hunting, lens breathing, exposure pumping when moving between harsh tropical sun and deep alley shade, occasional motion blur, subtle rolling shutter, mild digital compression artifacts, warm faded colors, soft contrast, slight sensor noise. No stabilization. No cinematic camera moves. No modern color grading.

00:00–00:02
Outside a small house entrance with an open terrace. She sits on the low terrace step adjusting her loose hair bun with both hands raised. A light warm breeze moves loose strands of hair. She smiles naturally while the camera struggles to lock focus.

00:02–00:05
The camera follows her into a narrow alley lined with potted plants and plastered walls. She notices a neighborhood cat resting on a warm concrete ledge, crouches down, and gently pets it. Framing drifts off-center as the operator tries to keep up. Morning sunlight flickers through banana leaves overhead.

00:05–00:08
Small front terrace with a glass of sweet iced tea on a wooden stool. She sits relaxed on a plastic chair watching the quiet gang, casually brushing hair behind her ear. A motorbike passes slowly in the distant background. Handheld side angle with natural camera drift.

00:08–00:10
She turns toward the camera with a warm, genuine smile, gives a gentle nod of acknowledgement, then turns to walk down the tree-lined alley holding her iced tea. Recording cuts abruptly to black mid-motion as if the camcorder was switched off.

Audio: Hyper-detailed environmental ASMR binaural audio — crisp micro-sounds captured up close with high sensitivity. Crisp rustle of cotton fabric and linen as she moves, deep rhythmic cat purring vibrating close to the microphone, ice cubes gently clinking against the glass condensation, soft scuff of leather sandals on textured concrete pavement, dry banana leaves brushing together in the warm breeze, rhythmic faint sound of a sapu lidi broom sweeping fallen leaves nearby, water dripping into a terracotta gentong, distant gentle crowing rooster, faint muffled low-frequency hum of a passing motorbike down the alley. Pure natural foley textures, zero music, zero studio effects, zero narration, zero speech.

Goal: Authentic Indonesian neighborhood life captured like a forgotten home video from the early 2000s — candid, imperfect, realistic, warm, and deeply believable, with immersive environmental ASMR soundscape.

A gritty, cinematic, photorealistic 20-second urban martial-arts action scene in 16:9, in the style of a 1990s Hong Kong / Korean crime-thriller. Handheld camera with sharp whip pans, rapid cuts, shaky close-ups, motion blur and flying debris. Color grade: cold blue-grey haze, desaturated teal and brown tones, deep shadows, a faint film grain, thick cigarette smoke drifting through the air, flat fluorescent tube lighting and a few warm amber lamps.

SETTING: A run-down underground mahjong parlor in an old building: stained concrete floor littered with broken glass and mahjong tiles, scuffed wood-paneled walls with framed pictures, green-felt mahjong tables with white tiles, green glass soju bottles, wooden chairs, a brown leather sofa, an old CRT television on a wooden stand glowing white static, ceiling fluorescent tubes, smoke and dust in the air.

HERO: A young woman with brown shoulder-length wavy hair in a loose, messy ponytail with bangs, wearing a black turtleneck, a worn black leather jacket and black trousers. Fierce, calm, cold expression. She fights with precise, powerful strikes, kicks and throws, using chairs and bottles.

THUGS: A gang of men in black leather jackets and dark clothes. The first is a heavyset, bearded man with short black hair, a big grin and a metal dog-tag chain around his neck.

Shot 1 (0–2s): Medium close-up. The big bearded thug grins and reaches out his hand toward her face to touch her chin. She stands with her back partly to the camera, head turned toward him. His face goes from a leer to alarm as she hits back.
Shot 2 (2–3s): The thug is flung backward with his arms flailing above his head, falling toward a mahjong table in the background.
Shot 3 (3–4s): He crashes into the green felt mahjong table, a bottle and white mahjong tiles exploding into the air around him.
Shot 4 (4–5s): Close-up: the woman turns to the camera, face cold and focused, hair strands in front of her face. Behind her another thug grins and swings a wooden chair at her.
Shot 5 (5–6.5s): She ducks under the chair, which smashes against a wall in a burst of splinters. Fast whip pan to a thug thrown against the wall, then a low shot of his legs kicking and another man sliding across the floor.
Shot 6 (6.5–8s): Wide shot: an old CRT TV in the background, a thug runs in and she sweeps him down; he slams across the edge of a mahjong table with tiles scattering. A thug crawls on the floor.
Shot 7 (8–9s): A grinning thug lunges at her with a green bottle. She dodges, grabs his arm and smashes the bottle, glass spraying; close-up of her fist and the bottle breaking in a spray of liquid.
Shot 8 (9–11s): Rapid close-quarters fight: she punches a thug in the face, spins, and he is knocked backward; a thug lounges on a brown leather sofa, stunned, as another body passes the lens; she turns and kicks.
Shot 9 (11–15s): Chaotic brawl in the smoke: multiple thugs rushing in, handheld shaky camera, her ponytail whipping, punches and elbows landing, a man yelling in rage, then a low-angle shot of black trouser legs mid-kick. One thug grabs her by the shoulders; she throws him over her hip.
Shot 10 (15–16s): Low shot across the floor: a defeated thug lies on the glass-strewn floor, tiles and green bottles around him, her black trouser legs step past the camera.
Shot 11 (16–18s): Wide static shot: the woman stands in the center of the wrecked parlor, legs apart, chest heaving, rubbing her arm, her head lowered, bodies of the thugs sprawled on the floor and chairs, broken chairs, bottles, the old TV flickering white.
Shot 12 (18–20.5s): She slowly raises her head and walks toward the camera; the camera pushes in slowly from a medium shot to a tight close-up of her face, wet hair strands across her forehead, breathing hard, staring straight into the lens with a cold, defiant look.

Audio: heavy thuds, bone-crunching punches, glass shattering, wood splintering, mahjong tiles scattering, men yelling and groaning, a low tense drone with a pounding drum beat that cuts to near silence on the final close-up with only her heavy breathing and the hiss of the TV static.

A birthday worth remembering 🎂

Generated with seedance 2.5

Prompt
Create a 30-second ultra-realistic Korean birthday vlog set in South Korea in the early 2000s, filmed casually by a friend on an authentic consumer DV camcorder. It must feel like genuine home-video footage, not a modern video with a retro filter.

EXACT ORDER — 7 SCENES:

0–4s — GETTING READY: The same young Korean birthday girl gets ready in her early-2000s bedroom, fixes her hair, adjusts makeup, picks up her handbag and smiles toward the camera.

4–8s — MEETING FRIENDS: She walks through an authentic Korean neighborhood, meets the same 2–3 friends, they congratulate her and walk together toward a bakery.

8–11s — BAKERY: They enter a small early-2000s Korean bakery, browse cakes and choose one birthday cake.

11–14s — BUYING CAKE: A friend pays with cash. The worker puts the cake into a box and hands it over. The same cake box remains visible as they walk to the café.

14–23s — CAFÉ CELEBRATION: They sit in a cozy early-2000s café. The cake is opened, a candle is lit, everyone sings Happy Birthday, the girl makes a wish and blows it out. They clap and laugh. She then cuts the cake, places pieces on plates, personally gives a piece to each friend, watches them eat, then takes a piece herself. Keep the entire sequence clearly visible and in order.

23–26s — PHOTOS: After eating, they take group photos using a small early-2000s disposable film camera. No smartphones or modern digital cameras. They pose, the shutter clicks and they laugh afterward.

26–30s — EVENING WALK: They leave the café and walk through the same neighborhood as it becomes evening. The girl carries her handbag, a friend carries the empty cake box/bag, and they chat and laugh while the camera follows from behind.

CONTINUITY: Same girl, friends, outfits, hairstyles, handbag and cake throughout. No teleporting, identity drift, outfit changes, disappearing props or reordered actions.

ERA: Everything must look authentically early 2000s Korean—fashion, hair, bakery, café, cars, signs, furniture, cake packaging and technology. No smartphones, AirPods, modern cars, LED screens or contemporary interiors.

CAMERA: Raw consumer DV footage with subtle handheld shake, imperfect framing, autofocus hunting, exposure shifts, soft digital detail, mild CCD/DV noise, motion blur, compression and occasional awkward zooms. Natural candid behavior, realistic skin and movement. No cinematic stabilization, 4K sharpness, beauty filters, VHS effects or professional filmmaking.

FINAL FEEL: A genuine Korean birthday memory from the early 2000s, with every scene and action clearly shown in the exact requested order.
```

</details>

> **[17-18s]** It collapses onto the gravel shoulder, motionless.
>
> **[18-19s]** The second infected recovers, charging the driver; he braces and swings the wrench in a wide arc.

> **[19-20s]** The impact connects hard, sending it sprawling against the guardrail.

> **[21-22s]** It goes still, both men breathing hard, weapons still raised.

> **[28-30s]** Final shot: the conductor and driver exchange an exhausted nod, passengers slowly regrouping behind them under the open sky. Cut to black.

### University Lab Infection Turns Students into Zombies

> The College Lab Infection 🧟‍♀️ Seedance 2.5 on @wavespeed_ai Prompt: Create a photorealistic live-action zombie-horror sequence inside a modern university scie…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/17336ecf1069.jpg" width="600" alt="University Lab Infection Turns Students into Zombies">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-create-a-photorealistic-live-action-zombie-horror-sequence-inside-a-modern-univ-8c8bb77e9ccd?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @AIwithSynthia | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/AIwithSynthia/status/2106955772395659431) | **公開日:** 2026-10-05 | **ヒート:** 91

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（29 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Create a photorealistic live-action zombie-horror sequence inside a modern university science laboratory during a normal college practical session. The central character is a young adult Korean female college student wearing a white laboratory coat over a simple shirt, blue jeans and white sneakers. Keep her face, hairstyle, clothing and body proportions identical throughout. Surround her with several college students, laboratory benches, microscopes, glass test tubes, chemical containers, computers and scientific equipment. Use realistic fluorescent lighting, practical effects, handheld camerawork and believable physics.

The students are quietly working on an experiment when a sealed glass specimen container suddenly cracks. A dark infected substance escapes and attaches itself to the young woman near her shoulder and neck. She stumbles backward in fear as dark veins slowly spread beneath her skin, traveling across her neck and face. Her fingers begin twitching, her body develops violent tremors and her skin becomes pale and clammy.

Her frightened expression gradually disappears. She raises her head and her eyes slowly transform into deep cloudy-black eyes. She suddenly turns toward a nearby classmate and charges. He desperately backs away behind a laboratory table while she repeatedly slams against it, moving with unnatural strength.

The other students freeze before scrambling toward the laboratory exit. The infected woman suddenly becomes completely still, then slowly rotates her head toward them. Her dark veins pulse visibly across her face while her black eyes remain fixed on the students.

Another glass specimen container cracks in the background. A second infected student suddenly collapses onto the floor, convulsing violently as dark veins spread across his neck and face and his eyes turn black. Panic erupts throughout the laboratory.

Students grab backpacks, chairs and laboratory equipment while trying to escape. One student pulls another toward the emergency exit as the infected chase them through the narrow aisles. A heavy laboratory table is pushed against the door as a temporary barricade.

The survivors finally reach the hallway and slam the laboratory door shut. Red emergency lights begin flashing as they catch their breath.

Inside the laboratory, everything becomes silent.

The camera slowly approaches the small observation window in the door. The infected female student stands motionless in the darkness, still wearing her white lab coat and jeans. Her pale face, prominent dark veins and completely black eyes are clearly visible.

She slowly tilts her head toward the window.

Then she suddenly smiles.

Cut to black.

AUDIO: Realistic laboratory ambience, fluorescent hum, glass cracking, equipment falling, footsteps, frightened breathing, screams, alarms, convulsions, impacts and distant banging. No music or exaggerated sound effects.

STYLE: Ultra-realistic college laboratory horror, practical-effects infection, realistic skin texture, believable anatomy, natural handheld camera, grounded movement, realistic facial transformation, dark spreading veins, black/cloudy eyes, violent convulsions and unnatural infected movements, cold fluorescent lighting transitioning into red emergency lighting.

NEGATIVE: Excessive gore, graphic dismemberment, graphic eye gore, anime, cartoon, illustration, plastic skin, fake CGI, duplicated students, face morphing, distorted anatomy, extra limbs, floating objects, impossible physics, changing clothes, inconsistent identities, subtitles, text, logos, watermark.
```

</details>

> This video is crazy. Made with seedance 2.5 Prompt : Create a 60-second ultra-realistic cinematic Korean action-thriller scene featuring a young woman with stra…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/622f5c9aa42a.jpg" width="600" alt="Red-Scarf Agent's Showdown in a Dark Classroom">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-create-a-60-second-ultra-realistic-cinematic-korean-action-thriller-scene-featu-f31c9f4b3fc8?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Noor_ul_ain43 | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/Noor_ul_ain43/status/2104793144743108833) | **公開日:** 2026-09-29 | **ヒート:** 91

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（54 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Create a 60-second ultra-realistic cinematic Korean action-thriller scene featuring a young woman with straight black hair, a sharp, confident expression, and a distinctive outfit: a black oversized jacket, light-colored top, black pants, and a bright red scarf. She is inside a dimly lit classroom, facing an armed opponent. The scene combines intense gunfight choreography, acrobatic martial arts, dramatic close-ups, and stylish action-film cinematography.

SCENE 1 — THE INTRODUCTION (0–6 seconds)

Open with a medium close-up of the young woman standing motionless in a dark classroom. She looks directly ahead with a calm, intimidating expression. Her black hair frames her face, and her red scarf contrasts with her dark clothing.

Cut to an extreme close-up of her face as she slightly tilts her head and purses her lips, maintaining a cool, fearless attitude. Insert a brief close-up of a handgun resting on a nearby desk to establish the danger.

SCENE 2 — THE ATTACK BEGINS (6–12 seconds)

Cut to a wider classroom shot. An armed opponent suddenly approaches. The woman immediately lowers her body, shifts her weight, and prepares to evade.

Use a fast tracking shot as she moves forward, followed by a sudden camera cut to her opponent. The choreography should feel fast, fluid, and physically convincing, with realistic body mechanics and clear spatial continuity.

SCENE 3 — ACROBATIC FIGHT (12–22 seconds)

The woman launches into an explosive sequence of martial arts movements. She ducks beneath an attack, pivots, performs a high spinning kick, and uses rapid changes of direction to avoid her opponent.

Capture the action with a combination of low-angle shots, wide shots, and close tracking movements. Her jacket and red scarf move naturally with her momentum.

Show brief flashes of gunfire and bright muzzle flashes illuminating the dark classroom. The camera alternates between the woman's controlled movements and the opponent's reactions.

SCENE 4 — THE GUNFIGHT (22–36 seconds)

Escalate the confrontation with rapid exchanges of gunfire. The woman takes cover, changes position, and uses the classroom desks as obstacles.

Include dramatic low-angle shots of her moving across the room, quick close-ups of her focused eyes, and brief shots of the muzzle flashes lighting the windows and furniture.

At one point, use a dramatic ground-level perspective as she stands over the camera's viewpoint, holding her weapon with a determined expression.

Cut to a close-up of her hands holding a firearm, then return to a wider shot showing her movement through the classroom.

SCENE 5 — THE FINAL EXCHANGE (36–48 seconds)

The action becomes more intense. The woman moves sideways while maintaining awareness of her opponent. Show a fast sequence of evasive movements, sharp turns, and acrobatic kicks.

Use dynamic handheld cinematography, short whip pans, and occasional slow motion at the most dramatic moments. Include realistic motion blur without losing the clarity of the choreography.

The opponent attempts to regain control, but she continues moving confidently through the classroom.

SCENE 6 — THE CINEMATIC FINISH (48–60 seconds)

Slow the pacing briefly. Show the woman standing with a confident, almost playful expression. Her hair is slightly disheveled, and her red scarf hangs loosely around her neck.

Cut to a wide shot of the classroom as the confrontation resumes. The woman and her opponent exchange a final sequence of rapid movements, with one last dramatic dodge and counterattack.

End with a close-up of her face as she looks toward the camera with a subtle, confident expression. Finish with a final cinematic action shot and a sharp cut to black.

CINEMATOGRAPHY AND VISUAL STYLE

- Photorealistic Korean action-film cinematography.
- Cool blue-green color grading, dark shadows, and muted classroom colors.

IMPORTANT CONSISTENCY INSTRUCTIONS
```

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（21 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Create a single continuous self-filmed vlog recorded on a handheld action camera in 16:9 aspect ratio. The camera is always held by the anthropomorphic fox, creating a natural first-person selfie perspective with light wind-induced shake, occasional soft lens flares from sunlight filtering through trees, realistic focus pulls, and dynamic walking/leaning motion. No cuts, transitions, or cinematic effects. Include a subtle realistic digital timestamp in the bottom-right corner that progresses naturally throughout the video.
Visual Style: Bright modern 4K digital look with vibrant yet soft natural colors, warm late-afternoon golden sunlight mixed with dappled shade, gentle water reflections and soft mist rising from the stream, subtle water droplets or mist on the lens near the water, and crisp details on fur and leaves. Slight handheld energy but smooth enough for casual vlogging. Widescreen 16:9 framing.
Character: Ember, a 24-year-old anthropomorphic red fox (bipedal, human-like proportions) with soft fluffy reddish-orange fur, white chest and muzzle, bright amber eyes, pointed ears, and a long bushy tail that sways naturally. He wears a simple light denim jacket with rolled sleeves over a soft cream t-shirt and comfortable cargo shorts. Personality: curious, warm, slightly playful and soft-spoken.
Location: Starts on a wooden footbridge over a clear mountain stream in a lush temperate forest, surrounded by mossy rocks, ferns, wildflowers, and overhanging maple and pine branches. Soft green and golden foliage, clear water with gentle ripples and small fish darting below.
Sequence (exactly 30 seconds, continuous take with timestamps):
0:00 – 0:05
Ember lifts the camera, smiles softly at the lens with his ears perking up, and says in a warm, slightly raspy voice:
“Hey friends… you won’t believe this spot I found.”
0:05 – 0:13
He walks slowly along the wooden bridge, lowering the camera slightly to show the clear stream and small fish swimming just below the surface while continuing:
“The water is so clear… I can see every little fish moving around.”
0:13 – 0:20
He crouches carefully at the edge, reaches one paw toward the water without touching it, and watches the fish dart past, laughing lightly:
“They’re swimming right up to me. Hi little guys.”
0:20 – 0:26
He turns the camera to capture both his face and the stream, then sits on a flat mossy rock at the water’s edge, letting his tail curl beside him. He gazes at the water for a few seconds and says quietly:
“Moments like this make everything feel peaceful.”
0:26 – 0:30
He stands up, waves one paw at the camera with a gentle smile, and says:
“Thanks for exploring with me. See you next time.”
The camera naturally lowers a little as he walks further along the path beside the stream.
```

</details>

### Earbud-Wearing Heroine Moves Vehicles by Telekinesis to Repel Zombies

> This AI video looks like Marvel cut its special effects budget Created by Seedance 2.5 on @makeugc Do you think it should have been made more dynamic and given …

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/a96e127522cd.jpg" width="600" alt="Earbud-Wearing Heroine Moves Vehicles by Telekinesis to Repel Zombies">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-create-a-30-second-photorealistic-live-action-superhero-apocalypse-movie-scene-c82709fe5d51?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @ZentrixHQ | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/ZentrixHQ/status/2116844865279451613) | **公開日:** 2026-11-01 | **ヒート:** 90

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（242 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Create a 30-second photorealistic LIVE-ACTION superhero apocalypse movie scene. Landscape 16:9. Real actress, real stunt performers, physical city location, full-size vehicles, natural lighting, and seamless feature-film visual effects.

REFERENCE
Use only Image Reference 1, the supplied photograph of the adult heroine. Preserve her exact face, hairstyle, hair colors, proportions, outfit, footwear, and accessories throughout. Render any stylized details as real skin, hair, fabric, and physical materials. Natural pores, expressive eyes, individual hair strands, clothing seams and folds.

No video reference is supplied. Construct everything from this description.

STORY
A woman calmly faces a charging zombie horde. She inserts white wireless earbuds, starts her music, and clears the avenue with effortless telekinesis. She knocks back the front ranks, blocks a flanking alley with a taxi, sends a bus through the main horde, and collapses a damaged pedestrian skybridge onto the bus and remaining zombies. She walks away through a clear side street.

One continuous event, one coherent location, exactly thirty seconds. No survivors, rescue subplot, dialogue, or flashbacks.

LIVE-ACTION STYLE
Everything resembles a finished theatrical film. The heroine looks like a real actress on location. Zombies resemble stunt performers in practical prosthetic makeup. Vehicles and buildings have real scale, weight, texture, and photographic lighting.

Natural acting, realistic anatomy, cinematic editing, believable collisions, and physically convincing destruction. No gameplay framing, game-engine appearance, animated characters, plastic skin, repetitive combat animations, HUD, health bars, damage numbers, or targeting effects.

HER POWER
Her telekinesis is overwhelmingly powerful and completely effortless. Moving a bus is as easy as moving a feather.

Relaxed shoulders, steady breathing, small finger and wrist gestures. Never show strain, trembling, clenched teeth, exhaustion, bracing, or loss of balance.

She is quietly confident, with a faint smile and subtle head movements to the music. No exaggerated dancing.

Telekinesis is invisible. Show it through object movement, displaced air, disturbed puddles, and sliding papers. No beams, glowing hands, magical symbols, or luminous shields.

Objects retain realistic material behavior. Suspension extends when lifted; metal deforms; glass breaks; impacts have weight and inertia. Her power supplies the force without making objects look weightless.

Each action has clear cause and effect: she looks toward a specific target, gestures, and that target immediately responds.

LOCATION
A wide downtown intersection after rain, under overcast daylight. Wet asphalt, puddles, isolated fires, thin smoke, damaged brick buildings, broken shop windows, abandoned vehicles, scattered glass and papers. Visibility remains clear.

Keep this geography consistent:
- The heroine stands at the intersection, facing north along the avenue.
- The main horde starts thirty meters ahead, sprinting toward her.
- An alley opens eight meters ahead on her right. A yellow taxi stands beside its entrance, with a white delivery van farther inside.
- An empty city bus stands ten meters to her left, level with her, facing north.
- The intersection has at least twenty-four meters of clear space for moving and rotating the bus.
- The avenue provides eighteen meters of clear width along the bus's path.
- A damaged enclosed pedestrian skybridge spans the avenue forty meters ahead. Missing glass, sagging central section, cracked connections.
- Abandoned cars stand immediately beyond the bridge and will stop the bus directly beneath it.
- The side street on her left is clear beyond the parked bus. Once the bus is lifted away, this exit is open.

Establish these objects before using them. All vehicles and the bridge are empty.

The heroine advances only two short steps and stays near the intersection, far from the final collapse.

ZOMBIES
Dozens of distinct adult infected people in dirty, torn everyday clothing, with pallid skin, clouded eyes, and convincing practical makeup.

Aggressive, varied running: stumbling, splashing, reaching, and navigating obstacles. No duplicated faces or synchronized movement.

They never wait motionless during her attacks. Their delays come from knockback, falling, recovering, and negotiating wreckage.

The first wave creates time for the taxi action. During that action, the main horde visibly recovers in the background. They resume advancing during the bus lift.

Threats come only from the established avenue and right alley. No unexplained attackers appear behind her. Emphasize impacts without graphic injury close-ups.

EARBUDS
Small white wireless earbuds resembling Apple AirPods: rounded earpieces with short white stems pointing downward. Correct size and natural fit. No wires, headband, or glowing details.

She already holds one in each hand at the start of the close-up. She inserts both and taps the right one. No case or phone. They remain in her ears afterward.

TIMELINE

00:00–00:03 — ESTABLISH THE THREAT
Eye-level wide shot from a three-quarter angle behind and beside the heroine.

The first frame clearly shows her full figure in the foreground, the charging horde in the middle distance, and the open street separating them. Keep her off-center so she does not hide the attackers.

Immediately establish the taxi, bus, skybridge, and open intersection.

The zombies start thirty meters away and rapidly gain ground. She remains calm and motionless. Use a restrained forward camera move.

Sound: running footsteps, splashing water, growls, distant fire, rattling metal. No music.

No low-angle opening, ground-level view, feet close-up, or delayed reveal.

00:03–00:05 — EARBUDS
Cut to a stable frontal chest-up close-up.

Holding one white earbud in each hand, she smoothly inserts both, releases them, and taps the right one. Natural fingers and correct earbud placement.

She raises her eyes toward the attackers with a slight smile.

At the tap, environmental sound becomes muffled from her listening perspective. An original dark electronic instrumental track begins, its first strong bass beat landing at 00:05.

00:05–00:09 — FIRST STRIKE
Eye-level medium-wide three-quarter shot showing her, the leading attacker, and the front ranks.

The first zombie lunges with arms extended.

She raises one palm. He stops in midair sixty centimeters from her hand, feet off the road. His torso stops first; limbs and clothing retain brief residual movement.

After less than half a second, she flicks her palm forward.

He shoots backward into the horde. The same gesture sends a broad directional pressure wave up the avenue, knocking the front ranks ten to fifteen meters backward.

Bodies tumble and collide, obstructing the following ranks. Water fans forward and papers rush away from her.

Clearly show the newly opened gap. This buys time for the next action.

She lowers her hand and takes two measured steps. The fallen zombies begin recovering immediately.

00:09–00:14 — TAXI
A lateral tracking shot reveals zombies emerging from the right alley.

She glances right and turns her wrist upward.

The yellow taxi rises one meter. All wheels leave the road, suspension extends, and water drips from the chassis. The lift is effortless.

Briefly show her, the floating taxi, and the flanking group together.

She sweeps two fingers toward the alley. The taxi travels sideways, broadside first, driving the attackers back inside. It crashes into the white van and stops diagonally across the entrance.

Metal buckles, glass breaks, and the van shifts backward. The wrecked vehicles completely block the alley and remain there.

She stays outside the trajectory. The main horde continues recovering in the background.

Sound: suspension creak, breaking glass, heavy metal impact.

00:14–00:18 — POSITION THE BUS
Cut wide: heroine near the intersection, bus to her left, horde farther ahead.

She looks left and slightly raises her palm.

The bus lifts one and a half meters. Wheels hang, fittings vibrate, and water falls from the wheel arches.

She guides it diagonally forward into the open intersection, keeping its entire path clear of her.

A turn of her wrist rotates it ninety degrees. It finishes broadside across the avenue, several meters ahead of her.

Maintain at least three meters of clearance from her throughout. No intersection with buildings or street furniture.

Clearly show: heroine behind, bus ahead, zombies farther north. No zombie remains between her and the bus.

Her posture stays loose, with no lifting strain.

00:18–00:23 — BUS STRIKE
She makes a small forward push.

The bus accelerates away from her, broadside first. It descends to the wet road and continues sliding under telekinetic force.

Tires skid sideways. Lower metal edges scrape the asphalt, producing sparks and water spray.

It knocks down the approaching ranks and sweeps through the main group. Show convincing mass and momentum.

Briefly track from a three-quarter side angle so its direction and impacts remain readable.

It crashes into the abandoned cars beyond the skybridge. The cars crumple and stop it directly beneath the bridge's central span.

Establish the final position: bus under bridge, crushed cars beyond, remaining zombies on the far side.

No zombies have crossed the bus. They are only beginning to gather against it.

No fireball. Use deformation, glass, sparks, and heavy impact sound.

The heroine remains near the original intersection.

00:23–00:25 — FINAL TARGET
Over her shoulder, show the bus under the damaged bridge.

Zombies reach upward from the far side but have not climbed over.

She looks up and casually raises her hand, gently curling her fingers.

A brief structural insert shows existing cracks widening and damaged connections bending under her force. Small fragments fall.

Her face remains calm. The music builds.

00:25–00:28 — COLLAPSE
Return wide, connecting her gesture with the distant bridge.

She lowers her hand effortlessly.

The central span breaks free and falls directly onto the bus and the street immediately behind it. It crushes the bus roof and blocks the remaining horde.

Concrete, twisted steel, and the wrecked bus create one continuous barrier across the avenue.

The released span falls with believable gravity. Steel bends, concrete fractures, glass breaks, and dust expands around the impact.

Heavy debris stays in the distant collapse zone. Only the bridge and its damaged connections fail; both surrounding buildings remain standing.

She remains safely near the intersection.

The impact lands on the strongest musical accent: deep rumbling, tearing metal, falling masonry.

00:28–00:30 — EXIT
Eye-level medium-wide shot from inside the clear side street to her left, looking back toward the intersection.

She turns into it and takes a few confident steps toward the retreating camera. Her route is open because the bus has been removed.

The blocked avenue and rising dust remain visible behind her.

A subtle head movement follows the music. Hands relaxed, normal breathing, no fatigue. No additional earbud adjustment.

A faint breeze moves her hair. No heavy debris reaches her.

End at exactly thirty seconds on her forward movement. No extra scene or title.

CAMERA AND EDITING
Coherent live-action movie coverage, motivated cuts, and readable trajectories.

Use eye-level wide and medium-wide shots for major actions. Show the heroine, controlled object, and target together when possible.

Close-ups only for earbuds, facial performance, and brief structural details.

Maintain screen direction and recognizable landmarks. Controlled tracking, natural perspective, realistic motion blur, restrained impact vibration.

No constant shaking, random whip pans, spinning camera, extreme distortion, gameplay camera, or cuts that hide impacts. No prolonged slow motion.

LIGHTING AND MATERIALS
Soft gray daylight, natural skin tones, restrained orange fire reflections.

Realistic skin, hair, fabric, wet asphalt, dirty glass, heavy steel, and concrete dust. Preserve reference colors without making hair or clothing glow.

Telekinesis is supernatural; everything affected behaves like real physical material.

No cartoon rendering, plastic faces, excessive bloom, or game-engine surfaces.

AUDIO
Exposed apocalypse ambience until the earbud tap, then a shift into her listening perspective and an original dark electronic instrumental with strong bass and percussion.

Music continues through all cuts. Gestures and major impacts align with accents. Keep collisions and destruction audible above it.

No lyrics, dialogue, narration, or subtitles.

CONTINUITY
One consistent heroine matching the photo. Earbuds stay in place.
Objects exist before moving; wreckage persists afterward.
Zombies recover and advance instead of waiting.
The bus starts beside her, moves ahead, then attacks.
No zombies remain between her and the deployed bus.
The bus stops beneath the bridge.
The bridge falls before zombies cross the bus.
No teleportation, duplication, disappearing objects, resetting damage, extra fingers, or intersecting bodies.
Every major movement follows her gesture. She never strains or becomes tired.

No low-angle opening, civilians, rescue subplot, game aesthetic, interface, glowing outlines, text, logos, watermarks, captions, or end title.

The result must feel like a real actress in a cinematic superhero apocalypse film, with physically convincing destruction and seamless feature-film visual effects.
```

</details>

**作者:** @itsSaira_1 | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/itsSaira_1/status/2104064261005709551) | **公開日:** 2026-09-27 | **ヒート:** 90

Cinematic photorealistic sci-fi action sequence, moody desaturated color grade, dramatic lighting, hyper-detailed mechanical design, motion blur for speed, 4K film quality.

Scene 1: Wide low-angle tracking shot of a futuristic armored rider in black tactical exosuit on a black-gold angular sci-fi motorcycle, speeding on empty highway, overcast sky.

Scene 2: Close-up transformation shot — motorcycle's rear panels mechanically unfold like wings while riding at high speed, sparks of light on metal joints.

Scene 3: Low-angle hero reveal shot of a sleek black Batmobile-style supercar with scissor/wing doors opening, front LED light strip glowing.

Scene 4: Rear-tracking low-angle shot of two motorcycles riding side by side on the highway, wheels blurred with speed.

Scene 5: Rear pursuit shot of the black supercar driving with glowing taillights, a motorcycle catching up close behind.

Scene 6: Aerial drone top-down shot of a curving highway with the car and motorcycles racing, small flying drone visible above for scale.

Scene 7: Close-up action shot of the car's front wheel with sparks flying off the road surface, high-speed drift feel.

Scene 8: Interior POV shot from the car's dashboard, glowing speedometer, driving fast through a dark tunnel with lights streaking past.

Scene 9: Tunnel chase shot — motorcycle and car entering/exiting a concrete tunnel, wet road reflections, cinematic light contrast.

Negative prompt: blurry, low quality, distorted proportions, extra limbs, deformed hands, warped motorcycle frame, flickering lights, inconsistent lighting, cartoonish, low-res textures, watermark, text, logo, oversaturated colors, daytime bright sun (unless overcast), shaky unstable camera, jerky motion, unrealistic physics, plastic look, glitch artifacts.

### Seedance 2.5 Dance Clip Real Enough to Fool You

> Nobody actually thinks this is real, right? Seedance 2.5 gets uncomfortably lifelike, right down to the shadows. Upload your own photo, add the prompt, and you dance too. 10 seconds.

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/media/poster/seedance-269d1fc95820.jpg" width="600" alt="Seedance 2.5 Dance Clip Real Enough to Fool You">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-269d1fc95820?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @johnAGI168 | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/johnAGI168/status/2095025524586193105) | **公開日:** 2026-09-02 | **ヒート:** 90

**安定度:** 51/100

**再テスト:** MiniMax H3 Max 768p · 2026-09-07 · ⚠️ 劣化 (スコア 50.9) · [出力](https://media.goodcase.ai/retests/seedance-269d1fc95820/video-minimax-h3-768p-20260907-phase1/generated.mp4)

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（129 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
不会有人认为这是真的吧？😄

Seedance 2.5 也过于逼真了啊🫠连影子都那么真实

上传自己的图片，加上下面的提示词，你也能这么跳😂

Seedance 2.5 prompt 👇

Duration: 10秒
Aspect ratio: 9:16
Reference: @图片1，锁定主角面部、发型和人物造型，不额外描述或更换穿着
Audio: 真实城市夜景环境声、助跑脚步、强风呼啸、弹性装置低频回弹声、落地闷响、朋友欢呼；无对白、无旁白、无字幕
Overall style: 极限运动手机实拍，真实高空距离，强烈临场感，一镜到底，轻微手持与高速运动模糊，拒绝游戏CG感

GLOBAL CONTINUITY

Setting:
夜晚城市高楼屋顶，绿色直升机停机坪，黄色圆形标线，低矮水泥护墙。两栋高楼之间形成垂直街道峡谷，地面可见车辆、路灯和十字路口。

Lower apparatus:
街道中央设置一座巨大的圆形黑色弹性回弹装置，中心为深黑色弹性面，外圈环绕连续彩虹灯带。装置尺寸巨大，正对屋顶边缘，作为主角下落和反弹的准确落点。

Main subject:
@图片1为唯一主角。人物身份、面部和造型全程一致。

Bystanders:
三至四名朋友站在屋顶左侧，用手机拍摄并等待主角返回。不能抢占主体。

CAMERA CONTINUITY

全片为屋顶朋友手持手机拍摄的一镜到底。

摄影者先在主角身后跟拍助跑；主角跃下后，摄影者迅速冲到护墙边俯拍；随后镜头持续锁定主角，从垂直向下逐渐抬向天空；主角反弹回到屋顶后，镜头恢复平视。

不切镜、不瞬移、不更换视角。

STUNT ENGINE

动作闭环必须固定：

屋顶助跑 → 飞越护墙 → 高空下落 → 命中圆形弹性面中心 → 装置深度下陷 → 强力回弹 → 主角高速升空 → 空中团身翻腾 → 展体定位 → 双脚返回同一屋顶 → 屈膝落地 → 欢呼庆祝。

SHOT 1（00:00-00:10）高空反弹往返特技

Subject:
@图片1主角站在屋顶停机坪后方，正对远处护墙。朋友位于画面左侧拍摄。

Action:

00:00-00:01.50｜快速助跑

主角背对镜头，从停机坪中央快速冲向屋顶边缘。步幅逐渐加大，身体前倾，双臂自然摆动。

摄影者手持手机紧跟主角向前跑，画面产生真实脚步震动。朋友站在左侧举起手机，紧张注视。

00:01.50-00:02.30｜跃出屋顶

主角最后一步用力蹬地，身体越过低矮护墙。离开屋顶的瞬间，双臂向左右展开，身体稍微前倾，主动朝两栋高楼之间俯冲。

摄影者冲到护墙边，镜头快速向下俯转，始终把主角保持在画面中央。

00:02.30-00:04.80｜高空下落

镜头接近垂直俯拍。主角沿楼宇之间的空间快速下降，身体由前倾逐渐调整为面向下方，双臂保持展开以稳定方向。

随着主角下降，街道、车辆和十字路口迅速显现。巨型黑色圆形弹性装置从画面下方出现，彩虹灯环照亮周围建筑。

主角的下降轨迹必须准确对准黑色弹性面的圆心，不能偏向灯环或撞向建筑。

00:04.80-00:05.50｜命中中心

主角身体落在黑色弹性面正中心。弹性面受到冲击后明显向下形成深凹，彩虹灯环保持固定，装置不能破裂。

短暂压缩后，弹性面以巨大力量恢复原状，将主角垂直向上弹出。

必须清楚表现“接触—下陷—压缩—回弹”四个阶段，不能在接触前提前反向飞行。

00:05.50-00:08.20｜高速反弹与空中翻腾

摄影者仍站在屋顶护墙边。画面底部短暂出现摄影者的双脚，强化真实第一视角和高空距离。

主角从圆形装置中心高速升起，沿原来的垂直路线返回屋顶。摄影机从垂直向下持续向上抬起，跟随主角穿过城市楼宇之间的空间。

主角升空后收紧腰腹，双膝靠近胸口，双臂抱住小腿，形成紧凑团身，在空中完成连续快速翻腾。不要锁定具体翻转方向，以自然连续旋转为准。

主角接近屋顶高度时开始打开身体：松开双腿、抬起头寻找屋顶、双脚转向下方，双臂向两侧展开保持平衡。

00:08.20-00:09.40｜返回屋顶

主角高速接近镜头，从屋顶护墙外侧越回停机坪。双脚同时接触绿色地面，膝盖和髋部深度弯曲，以低位蹲姿吸收落地冲击。

落地必须具有明显重量：鞋底或脚掌接触、腿部压缩、身体下沉、轻微反弹恢复。不能站直着轻飘落地。

摄影者快速后退半步，避免主角撞上镜头，同时继续保持正面近景。

00:09.40-00:10.00｜真实庆祝

主角从蹲姿快速站起，兴奋地看向镜头，张口欢呼。屋顶朋友冲进画面，举着手机围过来，拍肩、挥拳、大声庆祝。

镜头因人群靠近产生轻微晃动，以真实手机自拍视频状态结束。

Environment:
高楼灯光、街道车辆和彩虹灯环保持空间位置稳定。下落和上升必须发生在同一条垂直路线，主角最后返回起跳的同一栋屋顶。

Camera:
24mm手机广角，一镜到底。开始为背后低位跟拍；跃下后变为越肩俯拍；回弹时由垂直俯拍连续抬升至仰拍；最后恢复平视近景。

Style:
真实夜间手机曝光，高楼具有准确透视和尺度。下落阶段风声增强，城市灯光形成自然运动拖影；主角返回屋顶时速度极快，但人体轮廓仍然清晰。

Constraints:
这是经过专业布置的虚构极限运动场景。必须完整显示圆形弹性装置承接并反弹主角，不能表现为无保护自由坠落。

主角下降和上升必须连续可追踪。回弹来自黑色弹性面的物理压缩，不是飞行、瞬移、绳索拖拽或超能力。

---
Negative:

改变@图片1人物造型，重新设计服装，服装漂移，面部漂移，人物替换，
中途切镜，机位瞬移，场景跳变，摄影者突然出现在地面，第三人称航拍，
主角直接飞行，悬浮，隐形绳索拉升，反重力，瞬间返回屋顶，
没有弹性装置，错误落点，落在彩虹灯环，撞击建筑，撞击护墙，
接触前提前反弹，弹性面没有下陷，装置破裂，装置变形消失，
下落方向改变，上升路线偏移，身体穿过弹性面，人物尺寸异常，
翻腾时四肢扭曲，多余手臂，多余腿，关节反折，身体融化，复制人物，
落地无重量，站直落地，脚部穿透地面，跌倒受伤，血液，严重伤害，
朋友提前挡住镜头，群众冻结，手机变形，高楼弯曲，车辆漂浮，
动漫渲染，游戏CG，塑料皮肤，廉价绿幕，过度光效，过度慢动作，
字幕，屏幕文字，水印，品牌标志，旁白，额外对白
```

</details>

**作者:** @AIwithkhan | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/AIwithkhan/status/2094997895187673489) | **公開日:** 2026-09-02 | **ヒート:** 90

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（15 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Use the uploaded reference image as the exact character reference. Preserve her facial identity, hairstyle, eye color, skin tone, makeup, body proportions, 

Style: Early-2000s MiniDV home-video aesthetic, photorealistic live-action, shaky handheld camera, slight autofocus hunting, natural exposure shifts, motion blur, imperfect framing, warm afternoon light, authentic everyday atmosphere.

A young woman from Korea wearing a fitted pastel tshirt spends a casual afternoon with her boyfriend in a quiet neighborhood. She films herself walking down the street, smiling at the camera while her boyfriend walks beside her. They stop at a small street-food stall, share a snack, laugh together, and continue walking.

She playfully steals his sunglasses and puts them on, making him laugh. They sit together on a low wall, talk and tease each other, then walk through a small park while she records little moments on the handheld camera.

As the sun begins to set, they stop under a streetlight. She turns the camera toward him, they exchange a warm smile, move closer, and share a brief natural kiss on the lips.

The camera stays handheld and slightly imperfect as they laugh afterward and walk away together.

Audio: Natural street ambience, footsteps, distant traffic, birds, casual conversation and laughter. No music.

Negative prompt: No subtitles, text, logos, watermarks, identity changes, outfit changes, duplicate people, distorted hands, unnatural movements, or AI artifacts.
```

</details>

### Rainy cyberpunk rooftop duel of silver flame and crystal blades

> made this with claude + seedance 2.5 prompt below 👇

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/72b2f7ccaf3b.jpg" width="600" alt="Rainy cyberpunk rooftop duel of silver flame and crystal blades">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-rain-soaked-cyberpunk-skyscraper-rooftop-duel-at-night-premium-semi-realistic-5c74882b9f13?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @whizz_xD | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/whizz_xD/status/2124775736351367301) | **公開日:** 2026-11-23 | **ヒート:** 89

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（14 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Rain-soaked cyberpunk skyscraper rooftop duel at night, premium semi-realistic 3D CGI, cinematic action film. Two adult women fight in one continuous space: a wet rooftop platform with a glass curtain wall on one side and a steel catwalk bridge on the other, neon billboards, metal railings, vents, hanging cables, puddles, bokeh city lights.

Fighter A, SILVER FLAME GUNNER: adult woman, long black hair whipping across her face, cold and menacing; torn grey-white upper jacket, flowing deep-red long skirt; dual pistols. Her power: silver-white liquid metal streams and platinum-white sparks only, liquid metal forming whips, shields and thrust jets around her, muzzle flashes of dense white-gold sparks, shell casings and spark trails on the ground. Gun-kata, slides, rolls, flips, aggressive pressure.

Fighter B, VIOLET CRYSTAL BLADE: adult woman, short black hair, sharp eyes, black cyber-armor bodysuit with mechanical limbs; one long slim katana plus several mechanical arms on her back, each tipped with a glowing blue-violet energy crystal blade. Her power: blue-violet crystal cutting trails and ionized violet sparks only. Precise, cold, ultra-fast multi-angle slashes, counterattacks, slicing bullets mid-air.

Their effects never mix: silver/white-gold for A, blue-violet for B.

0-5s: Fight starts on frame one. A slides in low firing both guns; B's crystal arms cross and slice the bullets into sparks. B zigzags low through the fire, slashes at A's face; A blocks with her left gun, metal sparks and violet shards burst. A back-arm blade grazes A's shoulder, tearing her jacket. A fires point-blank, B deflects with the blade but is knocked back half a step.
5-10s: A sweeps B's legs; B braces on mechanical arms and opens a ring of four crystal blades. A rolls behind her, shoots off one blade tip. B spins, top and bottom blades scissor in; A blocks with a liquid-metal arm shield and slides out across the wet floor in a spray of sparks and water, then fires into B's left mechanical arm joint: sparks and debris, the arm stutters. B lunges with a stab; A blocks with crossed guns and is pushed back, feet carving a long water trail.
10-15s: B leaps up onto steel beams, mechanical arms gripping like spider legs, dive-slashing from changing angles; A wall-runs firing, bullets sparking off beams. B's blade cuts A's thigh-side skirt. A rolls, fires both guns into the ground: a forward wave of silver flame blasts B back through a glass panel, glass shards and violet sparks exploding.
15-20s: B bursts out of the glass with a larger blade array, wave-like combo attacks; a back blade slashes A's left arm, armor cracks, liquid metal spills, A staggers. B goes for the neck; A jams the blade with her left gun and fires the right gun into B's chest armor, blasting her back so two mechanical arms snap a railing. A pulls all liquid metal into a spinning silver-flame vortex around herself.
20-25s: Both power up: a silver liquid-metal storm swirling around A; B's crystal blades spread into huge blue-violet wings. A dashes in zigzags firing; B's blades slice bullets and cut the silver streams, gunfire knocks blades aside, each impact sparks in its own colors. A fires at B's face, B dodges and slashes down, A lands a liquid-metal-coated kick to B's side; B spins away and her blades circle behind A.
25-30s: B fuses all blades into a spinning blue-violet drill and charges like an arrow; A stands firm and unleashes a forward torrent of silver flame and gunfire. The two forces collide in a storm of white-gold and violet sparks. A suddenly cuts her left gun, the blade slips past her side, and she blasts both guns into the blade-array core; the crystal blades shatter and fly apart while B's last slash tears A's right shoulder. A huge silver and violet shockwave flings railings, billboards and glass. The camera pulls high and dives back: A kneels on one side, shoulder wounded, silver flame still flowing; B crouches on the other, broken blades flickering.
```

</details>

**作者:** @Just_sharon7 | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/Just_sharon7/status/2112913908617158822) | **公開日:** 2026-10-21 | **ヒート:** 89

<summary><b>プロンプト全文（110 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Situations in 2050 be like

Seedance 2.5 on @DomoAI_ 

prompt

TITLE: POV — DRIVING IN 2050

Create a 10-second ultra-photorealistic futuristic video showing a beautiful young woman casually driving along a highway in the year 2050, while hundreds of autonomous flying vehicles travel overhead in perfectly organized aerial traffic lanes.

MAIN CHARACTER — STRICT IDENTITY CONSISTENCY

A beautiful 23-year-old woman with long, straight pastel-pink hair, delicate facial features, realistic fair skin with visible natural texture, soft pink makeup, and subtle glossy lips.

She wears:
A fitted blue denim sleeveless top.
A futuristic metallic silver choker necklace.
Bright blue statement earrings.
A black seatbelt across her chest.

Maintain her exact face, hairstyle, outfit, proportions, and accessories throughout.

SCENE 1 — 0:00–0:02 | INSIDE THE CAR
Start with a fixed dashboard-mounted smartphone camera facing the woman from the passenger side.

She sits behind the steering wheel of a modern luxury vehicle, driving casually.

Warm golden-hour sunlight streams through the side window, illuminating her pink hair and face.

She looks ahead at the road, then briefly glances toward the camera with a subtle, amused smile.

The vehicle interior is realistic, with black leather seats, a dark roof, and natural windshield reflections.

SCENE 2 — 0:02–0:04 | THE FUTURISTIC HIGHWAY REVEAL

Hard cut to a spectacular elevated wide-angle view of a massive multilane highway at sunset.
A dark luxury SUV drives along the nearly empty highway.

Above the highway, hundreds of futuristic autonomous flying vehicles hover and travel in tightly organized aerial traffic lanes.

The flying vehicles have compact aerodynamic bodies, metallic silver and dark-gray surfaces, and advanced concealed propulsion systems.

They move smoothly in synchronized formations, maintaining precise distances from one another.

Some fly directly above the highway while others stretch toward the horizon, creating a dense futuristic transportation network.

Golden sunlight reflects off their metallic surfaces.

SCENE 3 — 0:04–0:06 | BACK TO THE DRIVER

Hard cut back to the same dashboard camera angle.

The pink-haired woman continues driving completely unfazed by the extraordinary traffic system outside.
She rests one hand naturally on the steering wheel and looks toward the windshield with a playful, slightly impressed expression.

Sunlight shifts across her face as the vehicle moves.

Her facial movements are subtle, natural, and believable.

SCENE 4 — 0:06–0:08 | MASSIVE AERIAL TRAFFIC

Cut to a dramatic elevated tracking shot following the dark SUV along the highway.

The camera reveals an enormous formation of flying vehicles stretching across the sky.

Several vehicles glide directly above the camera while hundreds more move across multiple aerial levels.
The futuristic traffic looks technologically advanced but physically believable.

Below, the dark SUV continues along the sunlit road, appearing tiny beneath the immense aerial transportation network.

Warm orange sunlight, long road shadows, atmospheric haze, and realistic metallic reflections create a spectacular cinematic atmosphere.

SCENE 5 — 0:08–0:10 | FINAL REACTION

Cut back inside the car.

The same pink-haired woman looks toward the camera and gives a small, knowing smile before returning her attention to the road.

Golden sunlight illuminates her face.

End on her calm, confident expression.
CAMERA AND VISUAL STYLE

Authentic smartphone dashboard-camera footage for interior shots.

Spectacular aerial cinematography for exterior shots.

Realistic handheld micro-vibrations inside the moving car.

Natural sunlight, believable shadows, and atmospheric depth.

Photorealistic flying vehicles with convincing scale, movement, and reflections.

Smooth aerial tracking with realistic motion blur.
Warm sunset color palette.
Natural skin texture, visible pores, realistic hair movement.
Strong contrast between ordinary everyday driving and extraordinary futuristic transportation.
ON-SCREEN TEXT
Small, simple white text reading "2050" positioned near the top center throughout the video.
AUDIO
Natural road noise, soft futuristic electric propulsion hums, subtle wind sounds, and a dreamy futuristic electronic soundtrack.
NEGATIVE PROMPT

No cartoon rendering, no anime, no video-game graphics, no artificial CGI-looking humans, no face morphing, no changing hair color, no inconsistent outfits, no distorted steering wheel, no extra fingers, no unrealistic flying vehicle collisions, no chaotic flight patterns, no flickering objects, no unnatural facial expressions, no excessive neon lighting, no dark nighttime setting.

FINAL GOAL

Make the video feel like an authentic viral social media clip recorded in the year 2050, where flying cars have become so ordinary that the woman barely reacts to hundreds of them passing overhead.

#DomoAI #DomoAICPP
```

</details>
