```
EXACT VIDEO RECREATION PROMPT — 31 SECONDS

0–3s | Opening shot: Low-angle wide close-up of a wounded woman lying on a cracked stone floor inside a ruined Gothic cathedral. Dark horned monsters and twisting tentacles loom in the background. Slow cinematic camera movement.

3–5s | Rising movement: The woman pushes herself up from the floor, raising one arm as a bright white magical blade of light forms in her hand. Medium shot, smooth upward camera movement.

5–7s | Magical activation: She kneels and grips the vertical white energy blade touching the floor. A brilliant golden circular rune spreads beneath her, illuminating the stone floor. Low wide shot.

7–10s | Cathedral reveal: Camera tilts sharply upward, revealing towering broken Gothic arches, a shattered roof, moonlit clouds and dark creatures silhouetted above. Transition to the golden-armored warrior emerging through red smoke.

10–12s | Helmet close-up: Tight frontal close-up of a fully enclosed dark metal knight helmet, narrow black eye slit, engraved steel plates. Slow camera push-in.

12–15s | Golden knight reveal: Cut to a heroic low-angle shot of a knight wearing ornate gold-and-silver armor, carrying a large shield with a glowing golden sun emblem and a heavy war hammer. He stands inside the ruined cathedral.

15–18s | First magical attack: The knight raises his shield and releases an intense white-gold energy blast. Bright light fills the frame. Camera tracks the blast, with sparks and magical particles.

18–20s | Energy impact: The white magical blast expands into a brilliant screen-filling flash. Strong bloom, fast cinematic transition, brief motion blur.

20–23s | Demon confrontation: Wide side view of the golden knight standing firm while a huge reddish-black horned demon lunges toward him. Long claws and dark tendrils extend toward the knight. The wounded woman remains visible in the background.

23–25s | Demon attack: The demon sweeps its claws forward. A bright golden energy projectile travels across the cathedral between the creature and the knight. Dynamic lateral camera movement.

25–27s | Magical shield: The knight raises his shield as a large circular white magical barrier with intricate glowing runes forms in front of him. The demon attacks the barrier, creating rippling light and sparks.

27–31s | Final wide shot: Camera pulls back to show the entire ruined cathedral. The golden knight stands in the centre of a glowing golden rune circle, shield raised and hammer in hand. The demon recoils on the left, while the wounded woman remains on the floor behind him beside the vertical white energy blade. Smoke, floating embers, blue moonlight and golden illumination. Hold the final composition.

VISUAL STYLE: Photorealistic dark fantasy film, 16:9 widescreen, cinematic lighting, realistic armor and stone textures, cold blue shadows, warm golden highlights, volumetric smoke, dramatic contrast, consistent architecture, smooth camera movements, exact shot order and timing.

NEGATIVE PROMPT: Different shot order, altered camera angles, random cuts, changed framing, different cathedral, different character positions, inconsistent armor, changing shield emblem, extra characters, duplicated limbs, deformed anatomy, cartoon, anime, low quality, excessive blur, shaky camera, camera flip, 180-degree rotation, unnatural movement, floating weapons, text, subtitles, watermark, logos.

#AI #AIart #VisualArt
```

</details>

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/40eec7295c01.jpg" width="600" alt="A Girl’s Retro Selfie Diary on Seoul’s Shopping Streets">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-seedance-2-5-on-14a9d2540b85?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Just_sharon7 | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/Just_sharon7/status/2109218767620444331) | **公開日:** 2026-10-11 | **ヒート:** 89

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（81 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Seedance 2.5 on @DomoAI_ 

Prompt

CAMERA:
Early-2010s smartphone front-camera selfie vlog, filmed on an old iPhone 4 or Samsung Galaxy S2. The girl holds the phone at arm's length, occasionally switching to the rear camera to show her surroundings. Natural hand tremors, awkward framing, accidental finger covering the lens, shaky walking footage, imperfect autofocus, and sudden camera tilts. No professional camera movements.
VIDEO QUALITY:
Authentic 2010–2012 smartphone footage, 480p resolution, low dynamic range, washed-out colors, visible digital noise, blocky compression artifacts, slightly blurry moving subjects, rolling-shutter wobble, overexposed skies, and automatic white-balance shifts. Looks like a forgotten YouTube vlog uploaded in 2011.

CHARACTER:
MILA — a cheerful 22-year-old East Asian girl with a Korean-Chinese appearance, long straight black hair with side-swept bangs, naturally fair skin, soft pink lip tint, minimal makeup, and expressive dark-brown eyes. Wearing a loose pastel-yellow cardigan over a white graphic T-shirt, a short denim skirt, white sneakers, and a small brown leather shoulder bag. Her appearance, hairstyle, outfit, and facial features remain identical throughout.
LOCATION:
A lively Seoul shopping neighborhood on a sunny afternoon. Narrow pedestrian streets, colorful Korean storefronts, clothing racks outside shops, stationery stores, photo booths, flower stands, parked scooters, and pedestrians walking naturally in the background.

STYLE:
Spontaneous, playful, slightly chaotic 2011 YouTube lifestyle vlog. Casual conversations directly into the phone, unscripted facial expressions, awkward pauses, spontaneous laughter, and quick amateur cuts.
SHOT-BY-SHOT SEQUENCE

0–2.5s — The Flower Shop
Front-camera selfie. MILA walks past a tiny flower shop, notices a beautiful bouquet, and suddenly stops.

Dialogue: "Wait, these flowers are literally so pretty!"
She turns the phone toward the flowers, briefly losing focus.

2.5–5s — The Photo Booth

Quick shaky cut. MILA squeezes inside a tiny Korean photo booth. The camera is held too close to her face. She makes an exaggerated silly expression and flashes a peace sign as the booth flashes.

Dialogue: "Oh my gosh, why do I look like that?!"

She bursts into laughter.

5–7.5s — The Claw Machine

MILA stands in front of a small arcade claw machine, filming herself and the machine alternately. She presses the button, the claw grabs a tiny plush bunny but drops it at the last second.

Dialogue: "Nooo! I almost got it!"

She dramatically pouts at the camera.

7.5–10s — The Sunglasses

Quick cut to a sidewalk accessories stand. MILA tries on oversized heart-shaped pink sunglasses, turns the front camera toward herself, and tilts her head.

Dialogue: "Okay, wait... kinda iconic!"

She laughs and adjusts the glasses.

10–12.5s — The Unexpected Wind

Walking down the street, a sudden breeze blows her bangs across her face. She tries to fix her hair while still holding the phone. The frame tilts awkwardly.

Dialogue: "Ahh! My hair! Seriously?!"

She giggles, brushing her bangs away.

12.5–15s — Goodbye

MILA stops beside a colorful storefront, holds up her photo-booth pictures, smiles brightly, and gives a little wave.

Dialogue: "Best random day ever! Byeee!"

She reaches toward the phone screen. Her finger partially covers the lens, followed by an abrupt cut to black.

AUDIO

Authentic low-quality smartphone microphone. Distant Korean street conversations, footsteps, shop music, arcade machine beeps, camera shutter, occasional wind hitting the microphone, and slight distortion when MILA laughs. Her voice is cheerful, spontaneous, conversational, and slightly muffled.

IMPORTANT GENERATION RULES

Exactly 15 seconds, vertical 9:16.

Same character identity and outfit in every shot.

Natural selfie perspective, never cinematic third-person footage.
Realistic hand movements and physically plausible phone handling.

Natural dialogue timing and accurate lip synchronization.
No beauty filters, artificial skin smoothing, or cinematic color grading.
No subtitles, text overlays, logos, or modern smartphone aesthetics.
Preserve the authentic, imperfect, nostalgic feeling of an amateur 2011 street vlog. #DomoAI #DomoAICPP
```

</details>

### Early-2000s Seoul Couple's Afternoon Drive

> A Little Drive, A Little Love ❤️ Seedance 2.5 Prompt: Create a 30-second, 1080p ultra-realistic early-2000s consumer DV camcorder home video of a young Korean c…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/64e9f9e3ac3b.jpg" width="600" alt="Early-2000s Seoul Couple's Afternoon Drive">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-create-a-30-second-1080p-ultra-realistic-early-2000s-consumer-dv-camcorder-hom-a3fa022a7b4e?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @AIwithkhan | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/AIwithkhan/status/2104776214066188611) | **公開日:** 2026-09-29 | **ヒート:** 89

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（39 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Create a 30-second, 1080p ultra-realistic early-2000s consumer DV camcorder home video of a young Korean couple going for a casual afternoon drive through Seoul. The footage should feel like a genuine memory recorded by a friend, imperfect and spontaneous rather than a polished commercial.

=== CHARACTERS ===

GIRL: Young adult Korean woman in her early 20s, naturally beautiful, realistic skin, minimal makeup, long slightly wavy dark hair tied loosely with a few strands framing her face. Ll

BOYFRIEND: Young adult Korean man in his early 20s, natural hairstyle, realistic skin, relaxed expression, wearing a loose white T-shirt, dark jeans and white sneakers. Keep his appearance consistent.

=== STORY ===

She waits outside her apartment building with a small shoulder bag, checking her phone and looking down the street. Her boyfriend arrives in an older compact car and smiles when he sees her. She opens the passenger door, gets inside and playfully looks toward the camera as if embarrassed about being filmed.

They drive slowly through an older Seoul neighborhood. She sits in the passenger seat with the window slightly open, enjoying the breeze while her hair moves naturally. Her boyfriend occasionally glances at her and smiles while keeping his attention on the road.

They stop at a small neighborhood convenience store. She gets out, buys two cold drinks and returns to the car. She hands one to her boyfriend, keeps the other for herself, and they laugh together before continuing the drive.

Later, they park near a quiet riverside road. They get out and walk beside the parked car, talking casually. She takes a sip of her drink, makes a playful expression at the taste, and he laughs. She lightly pushes his shoulder and they continue walking together.

A gentle breeze moves through her hair. He notices a strand across her face and gently moves it aside. She smiles and looks at him for a moment. They stand close together beside the car, sharing a quiet romantic moment.

Near the end, she looks toward the camera and laughs shyly, then turns back toward her boyfriend. He steps closer, she smiles, and they share a brief, natural kiss. They pull back, laugh softly and remain standing close together as the camera keeps recording.

The boyfriend looks toward the camera and jokingly shakes his head while she hides her face against his shoulder, laughing.

=== CAMERA / DV FEEL ===

Raw early-2000s consumer DV camcorder footage. Handheld camera operated by a friend sitting in the back seat and later walking with them. Imperfect framing, subtle handheld shake, autofocus hunting, occasional exposure shifts, faded summer colors, soft digital detail, mild sensor noise, natural motion blur and occasional accidental zooms. No stabilization and no polished cinematic movement.

=== AUDIO ===

Built-in camcorder microphone only. Car engine, road noise, turn signals, window breeze, distant Seoul traffic, convenience-store door chime, refrigerator hum, footsteps, birds, neighborhood voices, fabric movement and natural laughter. Their dialogue should feel casual and partially imperfect like a real home video. No music, no narration, no subtitles.

=== REALISM / CONTINUITY ===

Keep the same couple, faces, hairstyles, outfits, accessories and car throughout. Natural Korean facial features, realistic hands and fingers, believable walking, sitting, driving and kissing. Hair and clothing react naturally to the breeze and movement. Genuine expressions rather than exaggerated acting.

=== NEGATIVE ===

No cinematic commercial look, no gimbal shots, no drone shots, no dramatic lighting, no beauty filters, no plastic skin, no CGI appearance, no anime, no cartoon
```

</details>

```
Watch Venice become a sphere

Seedance 2.5 on @FishCreativeHQ 

Prompt

[Image 1](image_1) is the face and identity reference. A young Korean woman in her early twenties with EXACTLY the face of @[Image 1](image_1) — same facial structure, same features, natural Korean skin, no glasses. Long pastel pink hair, wearing a 1940s teal-orange wool coat-dress with white collar, a brown leather satchel worn cross-body, grey knee socks, black leather shoes, holding a folded yellow envelope. Every shot of her matches @[Image 1](image_1).
1940s Venice at golden hour. Amber and teal cinematic grade, anamorphic 35mm film look, shallow depth of field, volumetric god rays, fine film grain. Inception-style folding architecture — the Venetian city curls upward and hangs inverted overhead like a mirrored ceiling. The woman always stays under normal gravity while the world folds around her. Continuous camera motion, no hard cuts.
[TIMELINE PROMPT]
0–3s: Lateral tracking shot along a stone quay by the Grand Canal at sunset. The young woman @[Image 1](image_1) in a teal coat sprints past camera, clutching a yellow envelope. Above her the entire city hangs upside down, mirrored — a man in a bowler hat walks a dachshund across the inverted street. A red umbrella drifts weightlessly between the two worlds. Pigeons scatter through the amber light.
3–6s: Camera follows behind her into a narrow Venetian alley, dry leaves swirling in her wake. At the far end the street peels upward into the sky, buildings stacking vertically like a wall of windows. Her footsteps echo between the stone walls.
6–9s: The camera slowly rolls 90 degrees. The alley wall becomes the ground beneath her feet; she keeps running, unfazed. The roll continues into a wide orbit as she races up a floating spiral stone staircase suspended in mid-air, fragments of terracotta Venetian rooftops rotating around her like a kaleidoscope.
9–12s: Extreme wide silhouette. She runs across the top of an arched stone bridge over a wide canal against the blazing setting sun. The mirrored city hangs both above and below the arch. On the inverted plane, a lone pedestrian walks the opposite direction. Birds burst across the frame in slow motion.
12–15s: Low angle looking straight up between two towering building walls, the sky a thin bright strip. She leaps across the vertical chasm, arms spread wide, coat and hair flying, satchel swinging. Camera holds on her against the sky. Wind roars.
15–18s: Handheld push-in through a crowded 1940s Rialto market street — vendors stacking crates of apples, laundry strung overhead between the buildings. She weaves between blurred foreground shoppers, running away from camera. An apple rolls loose across the cobblestones.
18–21s: Camera tilts up a grand brick bell tower — a Venetian campanile — as the surrounding city folds and curls around it. The woman appears tiny on the tower ledge, pauses against the sun, then steps off toward the rooftops. Bells begin to ring.
21–24s: Rooftop terrace garden framed by a rose-covered arbor. An elderly woman in a grey cardigan waters a bed of vivid flowers with a tin can. Behind her, Venice and the domes of St Mark's Basilica glow at sunset over the lagoon while the inverted city hangs overhead. The young woman balances along the stone balustrade, arms out, then jumps down onto the terrace.
24–27s: She holds out the yellow envelope. The old woman turns, sets down the watering can, and takes it. Both smile warmly at each other. Rose petals drift upward past them, falling toward the inverted sky.
27–30s: Camera pulls back and rises fast. The whole city folds and rotates until it becomes a top-down aerial of Venice's canals and streets curling into a sphere. The two tiny figures remain on the terrace. Slow fade to warm light.
```

```
Leaving work at five shouldn’t require stealth mode, but her boss made it a mission.

Seedance 2.5 0n @SJinn_Agent 

Prompt

SCENE CONTEXT

A 30-second realistic AAA third-person stealth-action game sequence set in a Japanese corporate office at exactly 5:00 PM on a bright weekday afternoon. The player character, NAGI, has finished her work and must reach the elevator without being stopped by her boss and given another assignment.

ACTIVE REFERENCES

[@Image1] = NAGI’s facial identity, if a character reference is supplied. Use only her facial appearance as the identity reference; replace any school clothing and background with the adult office character and environment specified below.

NAGI: Japanese woman, 25, long straight black hair with blunt bangs, white short-sleeve office blouse, charcoal tailored trousers, black leather belt, white minimalist sneakers, employee ID clipped to her waistband, small black work tote held close to her body.

Voice: light, breathy adult female voice, speaking Japanese in a hushed whisper. Keep her face, hairstyle, outfit, proportions, and accessories identical throughout.

CAST SEPARATION

Four distinct adult office characters appear, each with a different silhouette, age, and hairstyle so they read as separate people:

TEAM LEADER: man in his 40s, 170 cm, average build, full head of short black hair parted at the side, thin metal-framed glasses, white dress shirt with a loosened navy tie. Appears only beside the first desk cluster, checking his watch.
PAPERWORK COWORKER: woman in her 20s, 160 cm, slim, brown hair in a high ponytail, beige cardigan over a white blouse, carrying a thick stack of printouts. Appears only in the printer aisle.
BOSS: man in his 60s, 160 cm, stout and round-shouldered, completely bald with a shiny scalp and a fringe of white hair above the ears, round gold-rimmed glasses, charcoal three-piece suit with a company lapel badge, carrying a blue assignment folder. Appears at the corridor T-junction and remains in that connected corridor during the whiteboard sequence.
OFFICE ASSISTANT: man in his early 30s, 182 cm, lean and broad-shouldered, thick full black hair swept back, short beard, navy shirt with rolled sleeves, dark trousers, no glasses. Pushes the mobile whiteboard from the meeting-room alcove toward the elevator lobby.

The BOSS is the only bald character in the entire video. The OFFICE ASSISTANT keeps a full head of thick black hair and a beard, stands more than 20 cm taller than the BOSS, and is clearly a different, much younger man.

LOCATION MAP

One unbroken spatial route the camera travels with her:

Her workstation → low desk partition → printer aisle → corridor T-junction → meeting-room alcove with mobile whiteboard → elevator lobby → elevator interior.

Keep the route compact and physically traversable within the 27-second continuous section.

Foreground: polished linoleum floor, office desks, steel filing cabinets, rolling chairs, and reflective glass meeting-room partitions.

Background: window wall on camera-right overlooking neighboring office buildings, dust suspended in the sunbeams. Light enters from camera-right along the office route.

ELEVATOR GEOMETRY

The elevator entrance is at the end of the office corridor. The call button is on the wall beside the entrance. The elevator is empty when NAGI enters.

Inside the elevator: brushed-metal rear wall, side handrails, softly illuminated ceiling, control panel beside the doorway. No rear exit and no mirror creating a second NAGI.

NAGI enters facing the rear wall, then turns to face the open elevator doors.

For the final reverse shot, the camera is positioned INSIDE the elevator, immediately beside the doorway, looking inward at NAGI. The brushed-metal rear wall is behind her. The elevator doors close behind the camera, allowing her face to remain visible throughout the final shot.

FIRST FRAME / BLOCKING

First frame is already in motion: NAGI darts in from lower-left toward camera-right, work tote clutched to her chest, and drops into a crouch-slide behind a low desk partition beside her workstation.

Camera behind her right shoulder. Game HUD fully visible from frame one.

A small desk clock reads 17:00.

FORMAT MODE

One continuous unbroken take from 0.0s to 27.0s with no cuts of any kind, then a single HARD CUT at 27.0s to one final locked reverse shot that runs to 30.0s.

Exactly one cut exists in the whole video, at 27.0s. The camera does not cut anywhere else.

OPTICS

One lens for the continuous section: 47° third-person over-the-shoulder framing as the baseline, breathing in to 29° on reaction beats and out to 63° for the boss encounter and moving-whiteboard concealment.

All FOV changes in that section happen as smooth continuous zoom breathing, never as a jump.

The final shot after the cut is a fixed 29° frontal framing of NAGI, no drift.

CAMERA

For the continuous section, the camera is a physical object flying with her.

Chest-height, 1.5 meters behind NAGI, slightly camera-right of her spine, swinging with a soft spring delay half a beat behind her turns like an analog-stick gameplay camera.

It dips to knee height when she crouches, rises when she stands, slides sideways along the filing cabinets with her, swings wide to corridor-left to reveal the boss encounter, drifts behind her for the moving whiteboard, and follows her through the open elevator doorway.

The camera travels through open space around desks and partitions. It never clips through furniture, walls, the whiteboard, or elevator doors.

After the cut, the camera is repositioned inside the elevator beside the doorway, looking inward at NAGI — locked off, eye level, completely static, no movement.

ACTION

[Beat 1 — 0.0s to 6.0s]

NAGI crouch-slides behind the low desk partition, back flat against it, chest heaving. She leans out 20 cm to peek at the TEAM LEADER checking his watch beside the adjacent desk cluster, then pulls back.

She whispers in Japanese:

{定時だ…捕まったら終わり。}

HUD flashes 【5:00 PM — SHIFT OVER】 and the mission banner 【OBJECTIVE: REACH THE ELEVATOR UNDETECTED】.

The TEAM LEADER turns toward a ringing desk phone. NAGI slips behind his back, staying below the desk-partition line, and moves into the printer aisle.

The camera follows without stopping.

[Beat 2 — 6.0s to 12.0s]

The camera stays with her beside the steel filing cabinets.

NAGI presses her spine flat against the cabinets and shuffles sideways at 2 km/h, sneakers rolling heel-to-toe in silence. The camera tracks sideways with her at the same speed.

The PAPERWORK COWORKER walks past the end of the row carrying a thick stack of printouts, scanning the office for someone available to help.

NAGI holds her breath, pupils tracking the coworker without moving her head, and whispers:

{来ないで…こっち見ないで。}

The minimap vision cone sweeps past NAGI’s marker and turns away.

HUD shows 【SNEAKING MODE】.

The PAPERWORK COWORKER exits frame-left. NAGI exhales, crouches beneath the edge of the last desk, and slips into the corridor with the camera still on her shoulder.

[Beat 3 — 12.0s to 18.0s]

The BOSS steps around the T-junction two meters in front of her, blue assignment folder in hand.

He catches a brief glimpse of movement. A red exclamation marker snaps above his bald head with a sharp alert sting. The HUD edge pulses red: 【ALERT】.

He starts saying in Japanese:

{なぎさん、追加の仕事が…}

The camera swings wide to corridor-left as NAGI reacts in one motion: hugs her tote tightly against her ribs, drops below his eye line, pivots around the corner of a tall filing cabinet, and slips into the adjacent meeting-room alcove.

The cabinet fully blocks his view before he can finish approaching her.

She whispers:

{今だけは無理…！}

HUD prints 【EVASIVE MANEUVER】 and 【BREAK LINE OF SIGHT】.

The BOSS takes a step toward the place where he last saw her, looking around the cabinet. NAGI has already moved to the far side of a mobile whiteboard in the alcove.

The camera falls in behind her again.

[Beat 4 — 18.0s to 24.0s]

The OFFICE ASSISTANT starts pushing the mobile whiteboard out of the meeting-room alcove toward the elevator lobby.

The whiteboard is a tall, opaque rectangular board on a metal frame with four caster wheels. Its panel extends low enough to conceal NAGI’s crouched torso and head; only her white sneakers occasionally show below the frame.

NAGI crouches on the side opposite the BOSS and shuffles with the board, matching its rolling speed exactly. She keeps her tote tucked close so it never protrudes beyond the board’s edge.

The camera tracks low on her concealed side, showing her careful steps and the BOSS intermittently visible beyond the board’s far edge.

The BOSS pauses, looks toward the moving whiteboard for a full beat, then turns back toward the office with his assignment folder.

From behind the board, NAGI whispers:

{ホワイトボード…最強。}

HUD prints 【MOBILE COVER EQUIPPED】, then 【ENEMY LOST CONTACT】.

The alert bar drains to green.

The OFFICE ASSISTANT steers the board into a wall recess beside the elevator lobby. NAGI slips away from its concealed side as it stops.

[Beat 5 — 24.0s to 27.0s]

NAGI reaches the elevator call button and taps it once.

A soft arrival chime sounds. The elevator is already waiting on this floor; its doors slide fully open.

She slips through the clear doorway. The camera follows her inside, staying behind her shoulder.

She turns to face the entrance and presses the door-close button once. Her shoulders remain tense, tote held tightly in both hands.

The doors remain open until the cut.

27.0s HARD CUT

[Beat 6 — 27.0s to 30.0s]

Locked static reverse shot from inside the elevator beside the doorway, looking inward:

NAGI stands facing camera, shoulders squared, work tote held in front of her, chest still rising fast. Behind her are the brushed-metal rear wall and a horizontal handrail.

Off camera behind the lens, from the distant office corridor, the BOSS calls in Japanese:

{なぎさん、ちょっといい？}

The elevator doors slide closed behind the camera. Their narrowing opening is registered by the shrinking patch of corridor light across NAGI’s face and the sliding-door sound.

NAGI answers brightly, looking straight down the lens:

{お先に失礼します！}

The doors finish closing. Her shoulders drop with relief.

HUD prints 【MISSION COMPLETE】, 【STEALTH RANK: S】, 【ADRENALINE +150】, and the final notification:

【AFTER-HOURS TASK EVADED】

Final frame: NAGI facing camera, a tiny triumphant smile pulling at one corner of her mouth.

PERFORMANCE

Photographic human realism indistinguishable from live-action footage: skin with visible pores, fine vellus hair catching the light, subsurface scattering in the earlobes and fingertips, capillary flush across her cheeks and ears from running, individual flyaway strands lifting off her hair, wet living eyes with true window catchlights and a faint red inner rim, clean matte skin.

Visible fast breathing at the collarbone, eyes darting a half-second before her head turns.

Tension shown by muscle: shoulders drawn up to her ears, fingers clamped white on the tote handles, jaw set, then a full-body shoulder drop on each escape.

All adults have equally real skin, real hair follicles, and natural asymmetric faces.

PHYSICS

Her blouse and tailored trousers crease naturally during crouches and turns. Hair lags and sweeps across her face on fast direction changes.

The work tote has real weight: its handles pull taut in her hands, and its body swings with inertia before settling against her torso.

The mobile whiteboard has real mass. Its caster wheels rotate against the floor, swivel when steered, and rattle lightly over a flooring seam. The board wobbles subtly when the OFFICE ASSISTANT starts and stops it.

NAGI moves with her own visible footsteps; the board does not drag or transport her.

The coworker’s printouts flex slightly while walking. The BOSS’s folder stays in his hand.

Elevator doors slide smoothly on fixed tracks and close only after NAGI and the camera are fully inside.

Contact shadows stay glued under every foot, every knee, every furniture leg, and every caster wheel.

LIGHTING

One continuous light logic across the office route: bright late-afternoon sun at 5600K entering from the window wall on camera-right, throwing long window-frame stripes across the floor.

NAGI passes through light and shadow as she travels. Soft bounce fill off the pale linoleum lifts her face. Fine dust hangs suspended in the sunbeams.

The elevator lobby receives the same window light. Inside the elevator, neutral overhead illumination provides continuous soft fill.

In the final reverse shot, the same corridor daylight enters from camera-left through the open doorway, raking across her cheek. As the doors close behind the lens, this daylight narrows and disappears naturally, leaving the elevator’s established overhead light.

Exposure rolls smoothly as she moves between bright and shadowed stretches, and the color temperature matches exactly across the cut.

HUD

GTA-style real-time game HUD locked to fixed screen positions for the whole 30 seconds, with every on-screen word written in English:

Circular minimap bottom-left with the player arrow and yellow vision cones for the office characters; health and stamina bars top-left; alert-state bar shifting green to amber to red; controller button prompts bottom-right; English mission objective text top-center; floating red exclamation markers over alerted characters; notification cards sliding in from screen-right.

The HUD stays in the same screen positions across the cut.

No subtitles or caption bars anywhere on screen.

The HUD is a clean flat graphic overlay composited on top of fully photographic live-action-grade footage.

AUDIO

Tense minimalist stealth synth loop with a pulsing heartbeat underneath, running unbroken across the cut and resolving to a short bright victory fanfare at the end.

Ambience carries continuously through the cut. Office sounds become naturally muffled as the elevator doors close.
All dialogue is spoken in Japanese.
STYLE
Photoreal live-action realism, real human beings and real fabric, natural skin tones with true-to-life color science, physically correct materials, realistic human proportions and anatomy, real optical depth of field, real floor reflections, subtle handheld gameplay motion blur, fine film grain at 5%, sharp detail intent.
OUTPUT SETTINGS
16:9, real-time speed throughout, one continuous 27-second take plus one 3-second locked shot after a single hard cut.
POSITIVE LOCKS
Exactly one cut in the entire video, at 27.0s — everything before it is a single unbroken take with the camera connected to NAGI.
NAGI is consistently an adult office employee, 25, with the same face, long black hair with blunt bangs, white office blouse, charcoal tailored trousers, black belt, white sneakers, clipped employee ID, and black work tote throughout, with dry clean skin.
The work tote stays with her throughout.
The mobile whiteboard appears only from the meeting-room alcove onward and is physically pushed by the OFFICE ASSISTANT. It remains between NAGI and the BOSS during the concealment beat.

The bald BOSS in the charcoal suit and the tall bearded OFFICE ASSISTANT with thick black hair are unmistakably different men.

The office route remains spatially continuous. Characters do not teleport between locations.

NAGI enters the elevator before turning toward its doors.

In the final shot, the camera is inside the elevator beside the doorway, with NAGI facing the lens and the brushed-metal rear wall behind her. The doors close behind the camera; they never pass between the lens and her face.

All on-screen text is English. The last 3 seconds hold a locked frontal eye-level shot of NAGI facing camera.

HUD elements stay in the same screen corners throughout.

One single NAGI on screen at all times.

All spoken lines are in Japanese.

Sunlight direction stays consistent across the office route, with a physically motivated transition to elevator lighting as the doors close.

Final notification: 【AFTER-HOURS TASK EVADED】.
```

### Three versions of the same cutscenes: hands up, night road, bar

> one PC made GTA 6 on my PS5 Pro look like a different game same console. same scenes. three versions: top: the original console output middle: the stream proces…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/4af3db4b306e.jpg" width="600" alt="Three versions of the same cutscenes: hands up, night road, bar">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/sergiokenzie-seedance-2-5-ai-447e95144fea?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @SergioKenzie | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/SergioKenzie/status/2125273692796039307) | **公開日:** 2026-11-24 | **ヒート:** 88

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（31 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
one PC made GTA 6 on my PS5 Pro look like a different game

same console. same scenes. three versions:

top: the original console output
middle: the stream processed on PC with DLSS 5
bottom: a cinematic AI remake with Seedance 2.5

watch the faces and lighting. then tell me it looks the same.

the PC version outputs 60 fps from the console’s 30 fps stream. those extra frames come from processing on the PC; the game still runs at 30 fps on the PS5.

Seedance takes it further. it recreates the cutscenes with realistic-looking actors and matches their lip movements to the dialogue. that bottom row is an AI remake, not playable footage.

my last video got 10M views, and the replies were still full of “looks exactly the same.”

i put all three versions together this time. judge for yourself.

want to try the PC setup?

1 → paste the prompt from the first comment into Claude Code. it checks your GPU and installs Veyra with the GTA 6 preset.

2 → on your PS5, open Settings > System > Remote Play and enable it.

3 → select Link Device and enter the eight-digit code in Veyra.

4 → go fullscreen and hold V to compare it with the original console output.

you’ll need a compatible PC. if you already have one, you can try this with your existing setup.

prompt in the first comment. full setup and requirements in the guide below.
```

</details>

### Diapered gamer collapses when GTA VI still needs 14 hours

> He waited 12 years for GTA 6 and still wasn't ready for what happened at 0 Created by Seedance 2.5 on @makeugc Watch his face the second the screen changes. And…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/98326ee65a29.jpg" width="600" alt="Diapered gamer collapses when GTA VI still needs 14 hours">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-cinematic-quality-mandatory-applies-to-every-shot-of-this-video-d1dab8446932?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Zyvex_0x | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/Zyvex_0x/status/2123082883556335933) | **公開日:** 2026-11-18 | **ヒート:** 88

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（59 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
👇

FORMAT: 22 seconds, vertical 9:16, one continuous handheld take, real time, no cuts. Authentic found footage shot on an early-2000s Sony DV tape camcorder: standard definition, soft optics, video noise, interlacing on motion, warm tungsten white balance, limited dynamic range, fine grain. It must feel like a real home clip that leaked online, never like a smartphone video and never like a movie. Where any line below mentions cinema cameras or lenses, this camcorder look overrides it: keep only the realism, physics, skin, texture and consistency requirements. The operator and the camcorder never appear: no hands, no body, no reflections.

CINEMATIC QUALITY (mandatory, applies to every shot of this video):
Generate this video as real live-action footage from a top-budget feature film. It must be indistinguishable from a real camera shoot and never look AI-generated, CGI-plastic or animated. Shot on ARRI Alexa 65 with sharp high-end prime lenses, cinematography in the style of Greig Fraser: motivated natural light, soft realistic shadows, true-to-life exposure, deep but detailed blacks, no blown highlights. 24 fps with natural 180-degree shutter motion blur on all movement. Native high resolution with razor-sharp focus on the subject, crisp micro-contrast and high-frequency detail on every edge, skin pore and surface texture. Subtle organic film-like grain over the whole image, stable across frames. Natural, slightly muted real-world color grade with accurate skin tones. Keep this quality consistent from the first frame to the last, with no flicker, morphing, warping or texture shimmer.

REFERENCES:
- IMAGE 1 (attached PS5 game page screenshot) is the TV screen only. It never affects the people, the room or the camera.

TV SCREEN: A real 55-inch LCD running a real PlayStation 5 shows IMAGE 1 full screen and static, with its exact left-aligned layout, artwork, colors and fonts: GTA VI icon top-left; "PS5" badge and the title "Grand Theft Auto VI: Ultimate Edition" along the top; the "VI grand theft auto" logo on the left; the description text; the white rounded timer button with a clock icon; the round "..." button; the "Friends Who Play" panel; "Official news"; the illustrated woman and man on the right half against a pink-orange sunset; the "R3 Health / Ratings / Privacy / Terms" bar bottom right. Only the timer button ever changes, in exactly two ways:
1. 0.0-3.0s it shows "00:00:06". From 3.0s the digits count down one per second: "00:00:05" at 4.0s, "00:00:04" at 5.0s, "00:00:03" at 6.0s, "00:00:02" at 7.0s, "00:00:01" at 8.0s, "00:00:00" at 9.0s.
2. At 10.0s the timer button disappears instantly and in its exact place appears a real PS5 download block: a large white "0%", under it a thin long almost empty progress bar with a tiny white sliver at the left and a dark grey track, and under the bar a small white line "14 hours remaining". Clean PlayStation-style sans-serif, perfectly spelled. It stays static until the end.
Filmed off the screen: slight LCD moire and a soft lamp reflection on the glass, but the timer and the download block stay sharp and readable.

CAMERA: His best friend films one-handed, about two and a half meters behind the gamer and to his right, camcorder held high at head height, angled slightly down. The whole TV is always visible, filling about two thirds of the frame width in the upper half; the gamer kneels low and to the left of the TV center, so his head never covers the timer or the download block. Two clumsy, overshooting zoom-ins: at 8.5s on the timer, and at 10.0s on the download block so "0%" and "14 hours remaining" fill the frame for about one second, then a jerky zoom back out. Amateur camerawork: hand shake, breathing sway, a slightly tilted horizon, late autofocus, auto-exposure pumping when the screen changes. The image shakes harder whenever the operator laughs.

REALISM: Ordinary people, no model looks. Real skin with pores, oily shine, stubble, sweat. Real physical emotion: a red face, bulging neck veins and spit on screams, wet eyes, a voice that cracks and goes hoarse. Objects have weight: the rug thuds under his fist. Light only from a warm floor lamp and the TV.

CHARACTERS:
- GAMER: about 28, average build, messy dark curly hair, light stubble. A plain black short T-shirt ending at the top of the diaper, NO pants: a bulky white adult diaper of matte cotton-like fabric with side tapes and a printed waistband, worn on bare skin. The diaper is never glowing, shiny, reflective or lit by the screen, only the same soft warm lamp light and shadow as his legs. Bare legs, bare feet. A black over-ear gaming headset. A WHITE PS5 DualSense controller. Obsessed, unhinged, loud, completely serious. A comedic, non-sexual look.
- OPERATOR: his best friend, heard only, laughing.
- No other people on screen.

SET (fixed): A small apartment at night. A 55-inch TV on a low dark wooden media stand against a beige wall, a white PS5 standing upright to its right. A warm floor lamp on the left. A grey shaggy rug. At the lower edge of the frame: an open pizza box with one slice of pizza, two unbranded energy drink cans and a bag of chips.

TIMELINE:
0.0-3.0s, THE SETUP: Timer "00:00:06". He kneels on the rug, back to the camera, the white controller gripped in both hands, rocking. The camera dips clumsily to the diaper, then back up to the TV. Operator, laughing: "Bro, are you wearing a diaper?" He, without turning: "I'm not missing a second."
3.0-9.0s, THE COUNTDOWN: He screams every number at the TV as it appears, louder each time, shoulders shaking: "SIX!" at 3.0s, "FIVE!" at 4.0s, "FOUR!" at 5.0s, "THREE!" at 6.0s, "TWO!" at 7.0s, "ONE!" at 8.0s. At 9.0s "00:00:00": "ZEROOO!", both arms raised, the controller high above his head.
9.0-10.0s, THE BEAT: Arms still raised, he freezes, breathing hard. Silence.
10.0-13.5s, THE DOWNLOAD: The download block appears. Zoom-in, then out. His arms sink slowly. Quiet, shaking: "Fourteen... hours?" He turns over his shoulder toward the camera, eyes wide and wet: "FOURTEEN HOURS, BRO!" The operator snorts. He points at the camera: "DON'T laugh."
13.5-16.5s, THE EXPLOSION: He turns back to the TV, face red, neck veins out, spit flying, pounding the rug with his free fist: "I WAITED TWELVE YEARS, SONY! TWELVE YEARS!" His voice breaks. The operator cries with laughter, the camera shakes.
16.5-22.0s, THE REASON: He goes quiet, slowly lowers himself sideways onto the rug and curls into a fetal position facing the TV, controller hugged to his chest, breathing hard. The download block stays visible above him. Operator, choking: "Bro... you good?" He does not turn his head, hoarse and dead serious: "Fourteen hours. That's why I wore it." The operator bursts out laughing, the camera dips. The picture stays lit to the last frame. End.

AUDIO & DIALOGUE: All dialogue in casual American English. The whole soundtrack must sound like an unedited real home recording made by the camcorder's built-in mic, never like AI voices, dubbing, ADR, voice-over or text-to-speech. Real people talking over each other in a small room.

VOICES:
- GAMER: a normal young man's mid-low voice, slightly nasal, raspy from shouting, casual American, a bit of mumble in quiet lines. Not a trained actor: uneven rhythm, words running together, real breath noise. His voice reaches the mic from about two and a half meters away and from his back, so it is softer, duller and full of room echo, with the high frequencies rolled off; it gets closer and clearer only when he turns toward the camera at 10.0-13.5s.
- OPERATOR: a slightly higher young man's voice, casual, about twenty centimeters from the mic, so it is loud, close, breathy and harsh, with mouth noise. He can barely talk through his own laughter: his words are squeezed out between laughs.

LINE DELIVERY:
- "Bro, are you wearing a diaper?": operator, mid-laugh, the word "diaper" breaking into a snort.
- "I'm not missing a second.": gamer, flat, fast, annoyed, not turning his head, almost mumbled.
- "SIX!" to "ONE!": each number a real scream, short and sharp, a fast inhale between them, getting louder and more strained each time; from "THREE!" on his voice cracks and distorts the mic.
- "ZEROOO!": a long full-chest scream, cracking at the end, heavily clipped.
- 9.0-10.0s: no words, only his hard, fast breathing and the PS5 fan.
- "Fourteen... hours?": barely above a whisper, shaky, a dry swallow before "hours".
- "FOURTEEN HOURS, BRO!": turned toward the camera, closer and clearer, a high desperate yell with a wet, tearful edge.
- "DON'T laugh.": gamer, sharp on "DON'T", then low and threatening on "laugh".
- "I WAITED TWELVE YEARS, SONY! TWELVE YEARS!": a full rage scream, hoarse, spit in the voice, the second "TWELVE YEARS" cracking and breaking into a sob, heavily clipped, overlapped by the fist thuds.
- "Bro... you good?": operator, choking, half a laugh, half concern, wheezing between the words.
- "Fourteen hours. That's why I wore it.": gamer, lying on the rug, quiet, hoarse, totally calm and dead serious, slightly muffled by the rug, a small sniff before "That's".
- The operator's laughter under everything: real and ugly, snorts, high wheezes, silent shaking laughter with only air, a cough from laughing; at the end an explosive laugh right into the mic that clips.

ROOM & MIC: Constant faint tape hiss and low room tone. A small carpeted room: short, soft reflections, no big echo. Handling rumble and plastic creaks whenever the camera moves or zooms; a faint zoom motor whine on each zoom. Loud screams clip and crackle. The PS5 fan whirs quietly the whole time, the TV buzzes faintly. The diaper crinkles softly whenever he moves; his knees scrape on the rug; dull heavy thuds of his fist on the rug at 13.5-16.5s; the rustle of his body settling onto the rug. No music, no sound effects that do not come from the room.

CONTINUITY: The timer reaches "00:00:00" only at 9.0s. The download block appears once at 10.0s and never changes or goes back to the timer. The TV and lamp stay on; the image never goes black. The TV is never fully hidden by his body. He faces the TV except for the turn at 10.0-13.5s. The controller stays in his hands. The headset stays on. The pizza, cans and chips stay in place. T-shirt and diaper the whole time, no pants. He ends lying on the rug.

NEGATIVE CONSTRAINTS: no cuts, no visible operator, hands, camcorder or phone, no gimbal or smooth movement, no slow motion, no music, no subtitles. No smartphone look, no HDR. TV: no redrawn interface, no centered layout, no different art, no timer jumps, no other numbers, no changing download numbers, no pop-ups, no misspelled or warped text, no screen hidden behind his head, no black screen. No glowing, shiny or lit-up diaper, no colored light on the diaper. No black controller, no pants or shorts, no nudity beyond bare legs. No theatrical acting, no model faces. Audio: no robotic, synthetic or studio-clean voices, no AI narrator tone, no perfect diction, no even volume, no dry close-miked studio voices on the gamer, no lip-sync delay. No other people on screen. No objects appearing or vanishing. No cartoonish, plastic or CGI look, no distorted hands or faces.
```

```
Create a ultra-realistic live-action revenge-comedy scene inside a Seoul subway station. Natural handheld camera movement, cold fluorescent lighting, authentic subway ambience, realistic physical interactions, fast-paced action, and spontaneous facial expressions. The scene should feel like a real Korean action-comedy film, not animation or a video game.

CHARACTER: A Korean woman in her 20s with a short black bob, sunglasses resting on her head, a fitted crop top, high-waisted blue jeans, black boots, and a brown shoulder bag. She holds an iced takeaway coffee and her smartphone while walking down the subway platform.

ACTION SEQUENCE:

0–4 seconds: A Korean man with messy dark hair featuring blonde tips, wearing a dark-green bomber jacket and black pants, suddenly slaps her across the face as he passes her and laughs mockingly. Her smartphone flies out of her hand, hits the tiled floor, and slides several feet away. She stumbles, catches herself against the handrail, and stares at him in disbelief. He turns around, points at her, and laughs even harder.

4–7 seconds: She slowly turns toward him, her expression changing from shock to cold anger. Without saying a word, she lifts her iced coffee and suddenly throws the entire drink directly into his face. Coffee splashes across his hair, jacket, and face, with ice cubes scattering onto the platform. His laughter immediately stops. He stands frozen, blinking in disbelief as coffee drips from his chin.

7–13 seconds: He angrily steps toward her, but she grabs the front of his bomber jacket, pivots, and uses a fast, exaggerated but physically convincing action-comedy throw to send him tumbling backward into a large metal rubbish bin beside a pillar. His legs briefly stick out over the rim as the bin rocks violently. He looks up in complete shock.

13–17 seconds: She walks over calmly, looks down at him, and says in a firm, icy voice: “Don't f* with me.”** She grabs the lid and slams it shut. The metal lid CLANGS loudly. The bin rattles as he struggles inside, then goes completely still.

17–20 seconds: She picks up her phone, checks the screen, brushes off her jeans, and walks away without looking back. Behind her, the rubbish-bin lid slowly lifts an inch as the man tries to escape, then drops shut again with a final metallic CLANG.

SOUND DESIGN: Authentic Seoul subway announcements in the background, footsteps, phone hitting tile, the man's mocking laughter, a wet coffee splash, shoes scraping the floor, a heavy metallic bin impact, and the final lid slam. Dialogue must be clear, natural, and synchronized with the characters' mouths. No background music overpowering the action.

VISUAL REQUIREMENTS: Maintain consistent faces, hairstyles, outfits, and location throughout. Show the phone falling clearly before the revenge begins. The coffee must visibly hit his face before she throws him into the bin. Make the entire throw readable in one continuous shot, with believable momentum and a comedic landing. The lid must close completely over him. Non-gory action, realistic skin texture, natural movement, restrained cinematic color grading, and authentic live-action footage. No supernatural powers, no excessive slow motion, no cartoon physics, and no scene transitions that skip the key actions.
```

```
Shot 6 — 26–30 sec

The tiny house is visible at the very top, emphasizing the incredible scale. Slowly pull the camera farther away until the mountain becomes surrounded by an endless sea of clouds.

End on a beautiful cinematic wide shot.
```

```
Seedance 2.5 on @Flovaai 

Use my referral link to join Flova https://t.co/l1lPYN3ENF

Prompt

Photoreal cinematic ~9 seconds, high-end fashion-film look. A young woman with long wavy blonde hair, olive-tan skin, sharp brows, and glossy lips. A pale pink flower is pinned behind her right ear. She wears a light blue spaghetti-strap mini dress with small black polka dots and carries a woven straw shoulder bag. Soft glam makeup, gold jewelry, warm skin highlights. Location: underground subway platform at night. Yellow safety strip on the platform edge, tiled walls, overhead fluorescent lights. A silver metro train streaks behind her in heavy motion blur — windows, doors, and at one moment an Japanese flag graphic on the passing car. Shot sequence, continuous energy: Medium close-up, she looks over her shoulder into camera while the train screams past behind her. Profile: she brings a lip wand or finger to her mouth, applying gloss as hair lifts in the train wind. She turns, hair swinging, eyes on camera, slight smirk. Wider on the platform: she touches her collarbone / strap, dress and bag readable, train still moving in the background. Head tipped back, hair flying, chin up, train with the flag ripping through frame. She looks back and smiles. Fast hair flip — hand rakes through hair, back partly to camera. Tight close-up: she blows a kiss to lens, fingers near lips, flower still in hair. Camera: mixed handheld fashion-cam and locked beauty close-ups. Shallow depth of field on her face, background train smeared into silver-and-light streaks. Practical station lighting plus a warm key on her skin. Color: creamy highlights, rich dark hair, the pink flower and red lips pop against cool subway metal. Motion: hair and dress always moving from train rush / her own turns. No extra people in focus. Mood: confident, flirty, summer-night city girl, music-video energy. Audio direction: upbeat dance-pop / Latin-pop, lyrics in the vein of “she got that vibe… dance all night… eyes on me.” Train rumble low under the track. Style keywords: photoreal fashion film, subway platform, motion-blur train, polka-dot sundress, pink hair flower, straw bag, glossy lips, hair flip, blow kiss, shallow focus, night metro lighting, 4K, no text overlay. Character lock (use with the reference still): Same woman 📷dp.jpg as the daylight field photo: long blonde center-parted waves, pink flower over the right ear, light blue black-polka-dot sundress, woven bag, warm skin, full lips. Keep identity consistent across every shot. #flovacpp
```

```
**CAMERA:**
DV 16mm tape camcorder handheld feel. POV of CHASE holding the camera herself, occasionally propping it on a rack or bench. Hand shake, misaligned framing, delayed focus pulls, clumsy zooms, occasional face cut-off framing, imperfect shots. Camcorder never appears on screen.

**LOOK:**
Soft, slightly blurry tape quality, faint tape noise, bloomed highlights under gym lighting, flickering auto-exposure, muted contrast, realistic skin tones.

**STYLE:**
Playful, self-deprecating gym-vlog tone — exaggerated complaining, genuine laughs, exhausted-but-amused energy. Quick handheld cuts, slightly wobbly camera movement matching her shaky legs.

**Character**

CHASE — Korean idol in her 20s. Long black hair in a high ponytail, glowing skin with a heavy post-workout sheen, big expressive eyes. Slim athletic build. Modest long-sleeve athletic top, loose joggers/leggings (arms and torso fully covered), sneakers, towel around neck, no jewelry.

**Setting**

A gym in the evening — squat rack, leg press machine, a water fountain across the room, mirror wall, soft overhead lighting.

**Storyboard**

1. *(~2s, propped camera at squat rack, medium shot)* She stands, hands on hips, catching her breath, dramatic exhale. CHASE: "Okay... leg day is not it today."

2. *(~2s, handheld, close on her face)* She winces, shaking out one leg, laughing at herself. CHASE: "Why did I do that many reps—"

3. *(~2s, medium propped shot, leg press machine)* She sits down, pushes through a rep, face straining, then flops back with a groan. CHASE: "Okay that's it, I'm done, I'm so done."

4. *(~1.5s, macro insert, shallow DOF)* Close-up on her hand gripping the machine handle, knuckles slightly tense. No dialogue — ambient gym sound only.

5. *(~2s, handheld, walking, camera wobbling)* She stands up and immediately wobbles, grabbing the rack for balance, bursting into laughter. CHASE: "Wait— okay my legs are literally shaking—"

6. *(~2s, handheld, unsteady walk)* She takes exaggerated, wobbly steps toward the water fountain, camera bouncing with her uneven gait. CHASE (laughing): "This is so embarrassing, someone help me—"

7. *(~1.5s, close handheld, at the fountain)* She leans down for a sip of water, sighs in relief, wipes her mouth with the back of her hand. CHASE: "Okay... water is life right now."

8. *(~2s, arm's-length selfie finish)* She leans against the wall, still catching her breath, grinning tiredly at the lens. CHASE: "Never doing legs again — see you guys, I'm going to go lie down somewhere."
```

**作者:** @ChillaiKalan__ | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/ChillaiKalan__/status/2119303534537822246) | **公開日:** 2026-11-08 | **ヒート:** 87

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（180 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
THE UNDERGROUND PARKING ENFORCER

30 SECONDS | 9:16 | 4K | 60 FPS
ULTRA-PHOTOREALISTIC KOREAN ACTION CINEMA
COMBAT SAMBO × DIRTY BOXING × SAVATE
6 SHOTS / EXACTLY 5 CUTS
ONE 0.25S MICRO SLOW-MOTION MOMENT

01 — ABSOLUTE CHARACTER & ENEMY LOCK

Image 1  — BLACK ROSE:
Preserve the exact reference face, pale natural skin, messy black updo, black fitted clothing, black floral embroidered outerwear, necklaces and heeled lace-up boots. Cold, rebellious tomboy attitude, relaxed half-lidded eyes and restrained arrogant smirk. No face, hair, body or outfit changes.

VICTIM:
Adult Japanese female motorcycle courier, approximately 26, short dark-brown hair, black riding jacket, dark jeans and motorcycle helmet held in one hand.

EXACTLY THREE ATTACKERS:

A — Tall muscular man, black leather jacket, aggressive leader.
B — Stocky man, gray bomber jacket, heavy punching style.
C — Lean man, olive-green jacket, fast grappler.

All three remain individually recognizable. No additional attackers, clones or replacement characters.

02 — FIXED GEOGRAPHY & ENVIRONMENT

Location: Spacious underground parking garage in Tokyo at night.

- SCREEN-LEFT: Black motorcycle and parked black sedan.
- CENTER: Female courier standing beside her motorcycle.
- SCREEN-RIGHT: Three attackers confronting her.
- BACKGROUND: Concrete pillars, overhead fluorescent lights, painted parking lines and a distant exit ramp.

Maintain the same 180-degree camera axis. Never mirror or reverse the geography.

03 — SIX-SHOT ACTION SEQUENCE

SHOT 1 | 0–5S — THE CONFRONTATION

CAMERA: 35mm medium-wide tracking shot from beside the black sedan.

The courier holds her helmet close to her chest. A blocks her path while B and C stand behind him.

VICTIM:
「通してください。」
Please let me pass.

A steps closer and knocks her helmet from her hand. It rolls across the concrete.

At SCREEN-LEFT, Image 1 closes the door of the black sedan and looks toward the confrontation.

CUT 1 — ON BLACK ROSE TURNING HER HEAD.

SHOT 2 | 5–10S — THE WARNING

CAMERA: 40mm low three-quarter tracking shot.

Image 1 walks toward the group with a relaxed posture.

BLACK ROSE:
「三人で一人を囲むの？」
Three against one?

A turns toward her with an arrogant smile.

A:
「お前には関係ない。」
None of your business.

A moves toward her with an aggressive right-hand swing.

BLACK ROSE slips outside his punch, redirects his arm and drives a compact body strike into his torso.

A folds forward and stumbles toward SCREEN-RIGHT.

CUT 2 — ON B CHARGING.

SHOT 3 | 10–15S — THE BOXER

CAMERA: 35mm lateral action tracking.

B launches a committed left hook.

Image 1 ducks underneath, pivots and delivers a sharp knee strike to his midsection, followed by a controlled shove that sends him stumbling backward.

B loses his balance and falls onto the clear concrete.

B IS DOWN — remains down.

C circles toward her from SCREEN-RIGHT.

CUT 3 — ON C REACHING FOR HER SHOULDERS.

SHOT 4 | 15–20S — THE GRAPPLER

CAMERA: 50mm medium close tracking shot.

C attempts a two-handed grab.

Image 1 pivots outside his arms, breaks his grip and uses a shoulder-control rotation to redirect his momentum.

C stumbles into a concrete pillar, rebounds and falls onto the floor.

C IS DOWN — remains down.

A struggles back to his feet in the background.

CUT 4 — ON A'S SECOND ATTACK.

SHOT 5 | 20–25S — THE FINAL COUNTER

CAMERA: 35mm low three-quarter frontal angle.

A rushes toward @Image 1 with a desperate straight punch.

She parries his arm outward, pivots and delivers one explosive spinning back kick into his torso.

ONE 0.25-SECOND MICRO SLOW-MOTION AT CONTACT.

Her boot compresses his jacket. His body moves backward in the direction of the kick.

INSTANT RETURN TO REAL-TIME.

A loses balance and falls onto the open concrete.

A IS DOWN — remains down.

CUT 5 — FOLLOW BLACK ROSE'S TURN TOWARD THE COURIER.

SHOT 6 | 25–30S — THE QUIET ENDING

CAMERA: 35mm slow reverse dolly.

Image 1 adjusts her embroidered outerwear and calmly looks toward the courier.

The courier retrieves her helmet and looks at BLACK ROSE with relief.

VICTIM:
「助けてくれて、ありがとう。」
Thank you for helping me.

BLACK ROSE gives a small sideways glance and replies:

「気をつけて。」
Be careful.

She turns and walks toward the black sedan.

Exactly three attackers remain down in their established positions.

04 — ABSOLUTE CONTINUITY RULES

ENEMY LEDGER:
A: Stumbles in Shot 2, falls in Shot 5.
B: Falls in Shot 3.
C: Falls in Shot 4.

FINAL COUNT: EXACTLY THREE ATTACKERS. All three remain down in Shot 6.

POSITION LOCK:
B lies near the center-right parking line.
C lies beside the right-side concrete pillar.
A lies farther toward the right-side exit lane.

No teleportation, duplicate bodies, disappearing attackers or rearranged positions.

CAMERA AXIS:
Maintain screen-left / center / screen-right geography throughout. Camera never crosses the 180-degree axis.

CONTACT PHYSICS:
Every strike requires visible contact, force transfer, realistic recoil and directionally correct balance loss. No floating bodies, instant collapses or impossible movement.

WORLD REACTIONS:
The victim steps away from the fight and stays safe. The fallen helmet remains on the floor until she retrieves it. Background remains stable.

AUDIO:
Natural Japanese dialogue, underground ventilation, footsteps, helmet rolling, clothing movement and realistic non-graphic fight impacts. No music.

NEGATIVE:
Extra attackers, cloned characters, incorrect eyelines, reversed geography, disappearing objects, distorted anatomy, excessive slow motion, blood, gore, weapons, subtitles, text overlays, cartoon physics, abrupt cut
```

```
The Shadow Dragon Awakens 🐉

Create an ultra-photorealistic Japanese dark fantasy action scene with Hollywood-level VFX and realistic physics.

A mysterious female shinobi with long black hair and a detailed black ninja outfit stands among the ruins of an ancient Japanese temple. Dark clouds, swirling dust, broken stone structures, and powerful winds create an intense atmosphere.

THE AWAKENING: She performs rapid ninja hand seals and shouts, 「黒龍降臨！」 (KOKURYŪ KŌRIN!). The ground cracks as a gigantic black dragon emerges, spreading its enormous wings and revealing glowing crimson eyes.

THE BATTLE: An armored demon charges toward her with a massive sword. The shinobi vanishes into black smoke as the dragon attacks, smashing ancient pillars with its powerful tail. Sparks fly when the dragon catches the demon's sword between its jaws.

THE FINAL ATTACK: The dragon gathers dark energy in its mouth and unleashes a devastating blast. A massive shockwave tears through the battlefield, sending rocks, dust, and debris flying.

THE ENDING: The defeated demon falls among the ruins. The dragon dissolves into black particles and merges with the woman's shadow. She calmly walks into the mist beneath a giant full moon as the camera pulls back for an epic cinematic ending.

VISUALS: Photorealistic characters, realistic dragon scales, natural hair movement, cinematic lighting, volumetric fog, detailed destruction, dynamic camera movements, and premium Hollywood-quality VFX.

AUDIO: No music, narration, or subtitles. Only wind, dragon roars, cracking earth, metallic impacts, and supernatural energy sounds.

NEGATIVE PROMPT: Anime, cartoon, cheap CGI, plastic skin, distorted faces, extra limbs, unrealistic physics, flickering, excessive glow, blood, and gore.

FORMAT: Vertical 9:16, consistent characters, smooth transitions, realistic motion, and maximum cinematic realism.
```

**作者:** @Adnan_Ai2 | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/Adnan_Ai2/status/2113451899013996748) | **公開日:** 2026-10-23 | **ヒート:** 87

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（13 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
**Long / Detailed Prompt:**

A warm, intimate scene continuing a dark-fantasy medieval drama, shot inside a rustic stone cottage interior with a prestige-television cinematic aesthetic — soft natural window light mixed with warm firelight, shallow depth of field, photorealistic creature texture work, handheld yet controlled camera movement, 16:9 widescreen, 24fps film grain, muted earthy color palette with warm amber accents.

**Scene 1 (0–6s):** Extreme close-up, low-angle shot on a rough-hewn wooden tabletop inside a dimly lit stone cottage. A small baby dragon — pale translucent pink skin, delicate frilled wing-like fins flaring from its head down its spine, large glossy dark eyes, soft rounded snout, and tiny sharp claws — crouches low on the table, sniffing curiously at a single gleaming gold coin resting in front of it. Faint dust motes drift through a shaft of soft light. Background softly blurred: a clay jug, a wooden bowl, and stone wall textures. The creature's skin has a faint wet sheen and subtle veining, anatomically detailed and grounded rather than cartoonish.

**Scene 2 (6–12s):** Wider shot reveals a young woman with long, dark, loosely tousled hair, wearing a cream lace-trimmed chemise beneath a dark woolen cloak, leaning across the wooden table toward the dragon. Her expression is tender, watchful, and slightly anxious — the look of someone hiding a secret and caring for it in private. A stone fireplace crackles warmly in the background, casting flickering amber light across the room, with a wrought-iron fire tool and hanging lantern visible nearby. A small leaded-glass window lets in cool grey daylight from the left, creating a natural contrast between warm interior firelight and cool exterior light.

**Scene 3 (12–20s):** The woman holds up a small torn piece of meat or bread between her fingers, offering it to the dragon. The baby dragon's eyes light up with eager attention; it opens its mouth to reveal small sharp teeth, its delicate wing-fins fluttering slightly with excitement, front claws gripping the wooden table as it leans forward to reach the food. Close-up alternating shots capture the dragon's playful, almost puppy-like eagerness and the woman's warm, affectionate half-smile as she watches it, clearly growing emotionally attached despite the danger of what the creature represents.

**Scene 4 (20–25s):** A final tender close-up — the dragon playfully snaps at the food, wings spreading slightly in excitement, while the woman smiles softly, her eyes reflecting the warm firelight. The mood is quiet, domestic, and secretly joyful, a brief moment of peace and bonding hidden away from the dangers of the outside world established in the earlier birth scene.

**Overall tone and continuity:** This is a direct continuation of the cave-birth dark-fantasy story — the same woman now secretly raising the dragon hatchling in hiding inside a humble medieval cottage, their bond deepening through small domestic moments like feeding. Visual continuity maintained: same character designs, same practical-effect creature texture (wet, fleshy, finely scaled, non-cartoonish), same muted cold-vs-warm cinematic color grading. Emotional core: forbidden maternal tenderness and quiet domestic intimacy contrasted against the creature's mythic, dangerous nature. Sound design implied: crackling fire, soft creature chirps/snorts, quiet ambient room tone. Aspect ratio 16:9, cinematic film grain, award-drama quality lighting and composition.
```

</details>

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/322498c2ac13.jpg" width="600" alt="Woman Playing Tennis on a Skateboard on a Pink Court at Night">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-create-a-photorealistic-cinematic-fashion-sports-video-set-on-a-pink-tennis-cou-b27f2c8361f5?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @AIwithNatalia | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/AIwithNatalia/status/2107056982163402833) | **公開日:** 2026-10-05 | **ヒート:** 87

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（12 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Create a photorealistic cinematic fashion-sports video set on a pink tennis court at night. The woman is standing and riding on a skateboard while simultaneously playing tennis. 
She wears the black-and-white tennis-inspired bodysuit, layered pearl necklaces, white leg warmers, sunglasses, and black high heels shown in the reference.  
She pushes off and smoothly skates across the tennis court on the skateboard, maintaining believable balance. She holds a tennis racket in one hand and hits tennis balls coming toward her while continuing to roll forward. 
Her movements should feel confident, stylish, athletic, and controlled—not exaggerated or cartoonish. Her long dark-red hair moves naturally with the motion.  
Shot sequence: 
0–3 sec: Low-angle close-up of the skateboard rolling across the pink court, wheels moving naturally as tennis balls bounce nearby. 
3–7 sec: Wide side-tracking shot showing her riding the skateboard across the court while returning a tennis ball with a clean forehand. 
7–11 sec: Smooth camera orbit around her as she continues skating and hits another incoming ball, with her hair and jewelry moving naturally. 
11–15 sec: Front-facing tracking shot as she rides toward the camera, performs one final stylish tennis swing, then smoothly passes the camera.  
Visual style: luxury fashion campaign meets futuristic tennis commercial, nighttime stadium lighting, glossy pink court, realistic shadows and reflections, cinematic depth of field, subtle slow motion during racket impacts, smooth stabilized camera movement, realistic human anatomy, realistic skateboard physics, natural facial expression, detailed skin and hair, high-end editorial photography, photorealistic.  
Format: 15 seconds, 24 fps, high resolution.  
Negative prompt: distorted face, changing identity, extra fingers, extra limbs, duplicated tennis rackets, warped skateboard, floating wheels, impossible body positions, broken anatomy, jittery motion, unnatural hair, flickering clothing, changing outfit, cartoon appearance, CGI-looking skin, blurry face, camera shake.
```

</details>
